In paired trials with pre-registered endpoints and exact McNemar tests, AAPT improves the success rate from 0.50 to 0.79 within a contested decision window ($p=1.8\times10^{-3}$), while producing no incorrect actions. Both open-loop and predict-and-replan baselines achieve zero success because they still decode during execution. A preparation-time sweep shows that the gain emerges where the latency-based tree-sizing rule predicts, and ablations reveal three key requirements: fast observer decoding, valid tree planning, and accurate branch routing. A pre-registered oracle probe rejects our initial hypothesis and instead points to branch routing as the causal bottleneck. We further reproduce the effect on an independent general-purpose multimodal model over 126 paired trials ($p=4.9\times10^{-13}$). On an external benchmark, AAPT matches the overall performance of a reactive baseline, although the two methods exhibit complementary strengths. Together, these results suggest that AAPT performs best when candidate actions can be enumerated in advance, whereas reactive execution remains stronger when they cannot.

</details>


### [104] [On-Policy and Off-Policy Learning for Large Action Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28408)
*Imad Aouali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本论文研究大规模动作空间下的上下文赌博机策略学习问题，提出了基于结构化贝叶斯方法的在线学习算法和基于潜在变量、悲观估计的离线学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在交互式系统中，当动作空间非常大时，传统上下文赌博机方法面临诸多挑战：在线学习场景下存在探索效率低的问题，离线学习场景下存在数据覆盖稀疏、重要性权重方差大、外推偏差和优化困难等问题，需要开发新的方法来有效处理大规模动作空间下的策略学习问题。

Method: 论文分为两部分：第一部分针对在线学习提出了两种结构化贝叶斯方法：meTS（混合效应Thompson采样）和dTS（基于扩散先验的Thompson采样），通过建模动作间依赖关系实现信息共享；第二部分针对离线学习提出了sDM（基于潜在变量的结构化直接方法），设计了凹的、可高效优化的策略加权对数似然目标，并开发了基于指数平滑和PAC-Bayesian边界的可微悲观方法来控制偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 提出的结构化贝叶斯方法能够有效共享动作间信息，其遗憾界取决于有效动作数量而非实际动作总数；离线学习方法在大规模动作空间中能够有效控制估计误差和优化误差，悲观方法成功平衡了正则化重要性采样估计器的偏差-方差权衡。

Conclusion: 通过引入结构化先验和建模动作间依赖关系，可以有效解决大规模动作空间下的策略学习难题，在线学习方法通过信息共享降低了有效动作空间维度，离线学习方法通过悲观估计和高效优化目标降低了估计和优化误差。

Abstract: This thesis studies policy learning in interactive systems where an agent observes a context, selects an action from a very large set, and receives partial feedback. The main framework is contextual bandits, with two paradigms: on-policy learning, where the agent interacts sequentially with the environment and minimizes regret, and off-policy learning, where it learns from logged data collected by a logging policy. In large action spaces, both settings face major challenges: inefficient exploration, sparse data coverage, high-variance importance weights, extrapolation bias, and difficult optimization landscapes. The first part develops structured Bayesian methods for on-policy learning. We introduce meTS, a mixed-effect extension of Thompson sampling, and dTS, which leverages diffusion-inspired priors to model dependencies between actions. These methods share information across actions and yield regret guarantees depending on an effective number of actions. The second part addresses off-policy learning. We propose sDM, a structured direct method based on latent variables, show that optimization error can dominate estimation error in large action spaces, and introduce concave, efficiently optimizable policy-weighted log-likelihood objectives. Finally, we develop differentiable pessimistic methods based on exponential smoothing and PAC-Bayesian bounds to control the bias-variance trade-off of regularized importance-sampling estimators.

</details>


### [105] [QQWorld: Quantile-Quantile Matching for World Model Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28415)
*Zhoushun Yu,Xiaoyu Hu,Xiangyu Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QQWorld提出分位数-分位数匹配目标替代Epps-Pulley目标，解决了LeWorldModel在控制重尾偏差时的梯度消失问题，提升了规划成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LeWorldModel使用Epps-Pulley目标将潜变量约束为各向同性高斯分布，但其矫正梯度在孤立尾部样本处快速消失，导致重尾偏差控制不足。

Method: 提出QQWorld方法：1) 用分位数-分位数匹配目标替代EP，直接将投影潜变量样本与秩匹配的高斯分位数对齐，在尾部保持有效矫正梯度；2) 开发跨批次QQ方法，利用历史批次的分离样本扩大有效排序池，并分析其偏差-方差权衡。

Result: 在四个控制环境中，QQWorld有效提升了LeWM的平均规划成功率，同时持续产生更好的高斯对齐和更薄的潜变量尾部。

Conclusion: 分位数-分位数匹配目标成功解决了潜变量世界模型中重尾偏差控制不足的问题，为提升规划性能提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Latent world models enable efficient planning by predicting future states in a compact representation space, but their performance depends critically on the quality of the learned latent distribution. LeWorldModel (LeWM) regularizes its latents toward an isotropic Gaussian using the Epps-Pulley (EP) objective. We show that the corrective gradients of EP rapidly vanish for isolated tail samples, leaving heavy-tailed deviations insufficiently controlled. To address this limitation, we propose QQWorld, which replaces EP with a quantile-quantile matching objective that directly aligns projected latent samples with rank-matched Gaussian quantiles, thereby maintaining effective corrective gradients in the tails. We further develop cross-batch QQ, which enlarges the effective ranking pool using detached samples from previous batches, and characterize its bias-variance trade-off. Across four control environments, QQWorld effectively improves the average planning success rate of LeWM, while consistently yielding better Gaussian alignment and thinner latent tails.

</details>


### [106] [QAdapt: A Noise-Adaptive Neural Pre-Decoding Framework for Quantum Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28422)
*Ran Miao,Rui Luo,Xiaohan Shan,Xiaoming Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAdapt框架，一种噪声自适应神经预解码方法，在表面码量子纠错中有效应对硬件噪声变化，降低逻辑错误率并提升解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算的性能不仅受限于物理噪声，还受到经典解码器处理快速生成的症状数据时的延迟限制。硬件噪声强烈、异构且非平稳，加上仿真到硬件的分布偏移会显著降低固定神经解码器的性能。

Method: 提出QAdapt，一种噪声自适应神经预解码框架，用于表面码量子纠错。该方法捕获症状数据中的局部时空相关性，在缓解灾难性遗忘的同时顺序适应演化的噪声条件，并将残差症状转发给传统的全局解码器。

Result: 在110种合成分布外噪声配置的旋转表面码存储电路中，QAdapt持续降低逻辑错误率。在Google Willow基准数据上，无需目标域微调即可实现逻辑错误率降低达5.79%，后端解码延迟降低达9.32%。

Conclusion: QAdapt提供了一种实用且与解码器兼容的方法，能够在演化的硬件噪声条件下提升量子纠错的鲁棒性和后端解码效率。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing (FTQC) relies on quantum error correction to suppress physical errors and preserve logical information at scale. In practice, however, performance is constrained not only by physical noise but also by the latency of classical decoders processing rapidly generated syndrome data. This challenge is exacerbated by hardware noise that is strong, heterogeneous, and nonstationary, as well as by the simulation-to-hardware distribution shift that can substantially degrade fixed neural decoders. We present QAdapt, a noise-adaptive neural pre-decoding framework for surface-code quantum error correction. QAdapt captures local spatiotemporal correlations in syndrome data, sequentially adapts to evolving noise conditions while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, and forwards the residual syndrome to a conventional global decoder. Across 110 synthetic out-of-distribution noise configurations for rotated surface-code memory circuits, QAdapt consistently reduces the logical error rate relative to the neural pre-decoding baseline. On Google's Willow benchmark data, without target-domain fine-tuning, it achieves reductions of up to 5.79 percent in logical error rate and 9.32 percent in backend decoding latency on the residual syndrome. These results demonstrate that QAdapt provides a practical and decoder-compatible approach to improving the robustness and backend decoding efficiency of quantum error correction under evolving hardware noise.

</details>


### [107] [Kohn-Sham Spectral Embedding on Sparse Graphs at the Nishimori Temperature for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28428)
*V. S. Usatyuk,D. A. Sapozhnikov,S. I. Egorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出KSSE方法，用稀疏图谱嵌入替代密集CNN分类器，在ImageNet-1000上以约21M参数达到88.93% Top-1准确率，超越Swin-L并匹配ViT-H/14，同时大幅减少模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统密集CNN分类器参数量大、计算成本高。本文受物理学的Kohn-Sham方法启发，旨在用稀疏图谱结构替代密集连接，在保持性能的同时大幅降低模型参数量和计算复杂度。

Method: 将预训练特征映射到准循环低密度奇偶校验图，构建正则化拉普拉斯算子作为Kohn-Sham哈密顿量；利用FFT和低阶Rayleigh精细化在O(N log N + k²N)时间内求解谱问题；提出星域手术优化图拓扑，通过分形分析认证从粗糙景观到星域盆地的转变。

Result: 在ImageNet-1000上使用冻结的EfficientNet-B4特征，KSSE以约21.24M参数实现88.93% Top-1准确率，超越参数量197M的Swin-L（86.4-87.3%），匹配参数量632M的ViT-H/14（88.0-89.5%），参数效率分别提升约10倍和30倍。

Conclusion: KSSE成功将物理启发的谱嵌入方法应用于分类任务，在理论上证明了六项结果（包括广义Ihara-Bass恒等式、陷阱集特征值对应等），实验上以极低参数量实现了与超大模型相当的性能，为高效模型设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: We introduce Kohn--Sham Spectral Embedding (KSSE), a physics-inspired energy-based model replacing dense CNN classifiers with a sparse-graph spectral embedding evaluated at the Nishimori temperature of an associated Random-Bond Ising Model. By mapping pre-trained features onto quasi-cyclic low-density parity-check graphs and constructing a regularized Laplacian acting as a Kohn--Sham Hamiltonian, we solve $D$ independent channel spectral problems in $\mathcal{O}(N\log N + k^2_{\text{mode}} N)$ time via FFT on circulant blocks (leveraging Pontryagin self-duality of $\mathbb{Z}/p\mathbb{Z}$) and low-order Rayleigh refinement. Graph topology is optimized using \emph{star-domain surgery}: rather than destroying information-carrying codewords by removing frustrated cycles, we construct edge shifts creating local convexity around codewords while bounding residual frustration to $ρ(B_γ)\leq 1+δ$. Multi-scale fractal analysis ($D_2$ spectrum) and fractal learning-rate landscape certifies a landscape transition from rough regimes ($D_2>3$) to star-domain basins ($D_2<1$), enabling Rayleigh refinement with $k_{\text{mode}}=5$ modes. We prove six theoretical results: a generalized Ihara--Bass identity linking belief propagation to the Laplacian; trapping-set eigenvalue correspondence; additive channel separability with an explicit exchange-correlation bound; a surgery theorem bounding frustration with attractor width $Ω(1/\sqrt{d_{\min}})$; a quasi-stationarity perturbation bound; and a fixed-point convergence theorem. In a transductive protocol on ImageNet-1000 with frozen EfficientNet-B4 features ($D=1792$), KSSE achieves \textbf{88.93\%} Top-1 accuracy using $\approx 21.24$M parameters, outperforming Swin-L (197M, 86.4--87.3\%) and matching ViT-H/14 (632M, 88.0--89.5\%) under standard inductive setups, while reducing model footprint by $10\times$ and $30\times$, respectively.

</details>


### [108] [Oracle-Budgeted Molecular Optimization with Short-Term Graph Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28437)
*Jiannan Yang,Veronika Thost,Xiang Ling,Tengfei Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出短期图记忆模块，通过图神经网络代理模型预筛选候选分子，在有限oracle预算下提高分子优化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子优化通常在有限的oracle预算下进行，这使得决定评估哪些分子与决定生成哪些分子同样重要。在预算受限的情况下，如何更智能地选择待评估的分子成为关键问题。

Method: 引入短期图记忆（short-term graph memory）插件模块，该模块保持生成器架构和原生更新规则不变，通过维护在线图神经网络代理模型来预筛选每轮的候选池，使固定的oracle预算用于具有更高预测效用的分子。

Result: 在标准分子优化基准测试中，应用于片段生成器时，在不增加oracle成本的情况下提高了top-10平均得分，且在任何oracle上都不落后于基准；在1000次调用的紧张预算下，四个测试生成器均获得提升。在更大预算下的收益与生成器的搜索广度和对oracle反馈的利用效率相关。

Conclusion: 提供了一种在固定oracle预算下更具选择性分配资源的方法，并展示了哪些生成器能从中受益，为分子优化中的oracle预算分配提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Molecular optimization is commonly performed under a limited oracle budget, which makes deciding what to evaluate as important as deciding what to generate. We introduce short-term graph memory, a plug-in module that preserves the generator architecture and native update rule while learning from previously evaluated molecules to prioritize subsequent oracle queries. The module maintains an online graph neural surrogate that pre-screens each round's candidate pool, so the fixed oracle budget is spent on molecules with higher predicted utility. Applied to a fragment-based generator on a standard molecular optimization benchmark, it improves the mean top-10 score at no extra oracle cost and never falls behind the base on any oracle; the gain extends to all four generators we tested at a tight budget of one thousand calls. We then analyze how surrogate-guided selection interacts with the exploration and exploitation behavior of different generators. Its benefit at larger budgets is consistent with two properties of the backbone: how broadly it searches, and how effectively its native search already exploits oracle feedback. We provide a simple way to spend a fixed oracle budget more selectively, and evidence on which generators benefit from it.

</details>


### [109] [Cybersecurity Detection Classification with Reasoning-enabled Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28460)
*Amol Khanna,Manu Nandan,Cristian Viorel Popa,Joan Pujol-Roig,Diana Bolocan,Laura Vasilie,Alexandru Apostu,Chase Helwig,Mihaela Gaman,Michael Brautbar,Edward Raff,Chase Midler,Sven Krasser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于思维链推理的SOC告警分类系统，通过结合自动提示优化、自训练和强化学习，在Windows端点检测数据上实现了82.6%的准确率，显著缓解了告警疲劳问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全运营中心(SOCs)面临严重的告警疲劳问题，检测告警数量远超工作人员日常处理能力。现有方法直接让大语言模型输出分类标签，但未训练模型对检测是否为真实威胁进行推理判断。

Method: 在真实人工标注的Windows端点检测数据上，结合自动提示优化、自训练和可验证奖励的强化学习，训练了一个思维链(CoT)推理增强的分类器；同时单独训练一个校准器，读取完整推理轨迹来估计判决正确的概率。

Result: 系统达到82.6%测试准确率；在高置信度操作点上，相比直接标签LLM分类器，良性召回率提升43.0%，恶意召回率提升18.3%；未训练的置信度判断器会将高置信度召回率降至零；微调的30B模型显著优于前沿通用模型。

Conclusion: 思维链推理可有效提升SOC告警分类性能，但会降低标签token概率，需要专门的校准器；针对性训练的模型比大规模通用模型更有效，验证了在安全领域进行专门训练的必要性。

Abstract: A major issue in Security Operations Centers (SOCs) is alert fatigue, as the number of detections reported is more than staff can triage in a given day. Prior work prompts or fine-tunes large language models (LLMs) to emit a triage label directly, but does not train them to reason about whether a detection is a genuine threat. We train a chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning-enabled triage classifier on real, human-labeled Windows endpoint detections by combining automated prompt optimization, self-training, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards. We find that CoT reasoning also degrades the label-token probabilities that automated triage relies on, so we separately train a calibrator that reads the full reasoning trace and estimates the probability that the verdict is correct. Our system reaches 82.6% test accuracy and, at the high-confidence operating point that governs automated triage, improves benign recall by 43.0% and malicious recall by 18.3% over a direct-label LLM classifier. We further show that the trained calibrator is necessary - an untrained confidence judge collapses high-confidence recall to zero - and that a finetuned 30B model significantly outperforms frontier general-purpose models, motivating targeted training over scale.

</details>


### [110] [Stage-Replay Divergence Follows the KV Cache: Fixed-Prefix Precision Controls and Bidirectional Cache Transplantation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28495)
*Alexander Boesgaard Lorup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过审计发现，在BF16精度下，中间前缀的重建与原始解码器状态存在差异，但这些差异可通过KV缓存的移植来传递；在FP32精度下则无差异。边界KV缓存是分歧轨迹的因果载体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Stage-replay诊断方法假设重建中间token前缀可以视作从原始解码器状态继续，但该假设在推理阶段边界处未被充分验证。研究旨在审计这一假设的有效性，探究KV缓存状态对模型轨迹分歧的影响。

Method: 研究使用Qwen2.5衍生系统，在完整推理阶段边界进行审计实验。采用200项匹配实验，比较保留的实时缓存与一次性预填充的差异，并设置精确副本作为对照。通过固定前缀的2×2因子设计，交叉对比构建方式（实时缓存vs预填充）和数值精度（BF16 vs FP32）。开发了一种增量式令牌缓存方法，并进行双向KV层移植实验。

Result: 在BF16精度下，副本保持精确，但构建方式在166个后缀和20个正确性标签上出现差异。FP32精度下未出现解码分歧（95% Wilson上界1.88%）。增量式缓存方法在12/12行上实现位精确匹配。双向移植48层键值层后，所有测试的分歧延续都跟随其缓存供体（主检查点24/24，后期检查点复制43/43）。

Conclusion: 精确令牌重放无需保留实时状态保真度即可实现可重复性。边界KV缓存是分歧轨迹的因果充分载体，数值精度调节其行为表达。研究结果对理解推理阶段的状态管理和缓存机制具有重要意义。

Abstract: Stage-replay diagnostics reconstruct intermediate token prefixes and treat fresh-prefill continuation as continuation from the decoder state that originally reached the prefix. We audit that assumption at a whole reasoning-stage boundary in a Qwen2.5-derived system. A matched 200-item experiment compares retained live cache with one-shot prefill of identical integer tokens and places an exact replica on both sides. In BF16, replicas remain exact while the constructions differ on 166 suffixes and 20 correctness labels; the accuracy difference is only one point (paired 95% CI [-3.5, +5.5]). A fixed-prefix 2x2 holds all 200 token states constant while crossing construction and precision. The BF16 disagreements recur, whereas FP32 produces no decoded disagreement (95% Wilson upper bound 1.88%). A prospective bridge makes token-by-token incremental and retained live caches bit-exact on 12/12 rows; an all-200 saved-ledger audit reproduces every retained trajectory and comparison fingerprint. Bidirectional transplantation of all 48 key/value layers makes every tested divergent continuation follow its cache donor, both on a selected set at the primary checkpoint (24/24) and an outcome-blind replication at a later checkpoint (43/43). Exact-token replay can therefore be repeatable without preserving live-state fidelity. On the tested states, boundary K/V cache is a causally sufficient carrier of the divergent trajectory, while numerical precision moderates its behavioral expression.

</details>


### [111] [The Role of Causality in Algorithmic Recourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28497)
*Srikanth Avasarala,Varun Gupta,Shahin Jabbari,Saber Salehkaleybar,Juba Ziani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种因果执行框架下的算法补救方法，解决了传统方法仅关注改变模型预测而忽略真实资质改进的问题，通过建模特征间的因果交互，实现了稳定的解决方案并减少了策略性博弈行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的算法补救方法仅关注翻转模型预测，而未考虑推荐的变化是否真正改善了个体的实际资质，还是仅仅实现了对分类器的策略性博弈。这种疏忽会导致部署的补救策略引发行为响应，降低预测准确性，并在模型重训练后失效。

Method: 作者提出了一个因果执行框架，通过结构因果模型建模补救行动如何在特征之间传播以及其对真实标签的影响。该方法将因果响应建模为非凸优化问题，并在标准凸损失下刻画了存在执行稳定解的条件，通过简单迭代动态高效计算这些解。

Result: 实验结果表明，忽略因果结构的补救策略会引发大型、不匹配的行为响应，而因果补救方法能够达到稳定的均衡状态，有效减少博弈动机。在半合成和真实信贷数据集上，该方法持续优于标准经验风险最小化，同时减少了因策略性代理行为导致的分布偏移所需的重复模型重训练。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了因果执行框架在算法补救中的重要性，为高风险分类场景（如贷款和抵押申请）提供了更稳健的解决方案。通过考虑因果结构，该方法能够实现稳定的均衡，减少策略性博弈行为，提高模型长期有效性，为实际部署提供了理论保证和实践指导。

Abstract: Algorithmic recourse aims to provide individuals with actionable changes to improve their predicted outcomes in high-stakes classification settings, such as loan and mortgage applications. However, most existing approaches focus only on flipping a model's prediction, without accounting for whether the recommended changes lead to genuine improvement in an individual's true qualifications or merely enable strategic gaming of the classifier. Consequently, deployed recourse policies can induce behavioral responses that degrade predictive accuracy and become ineffective after model retraining.
  In this work, we formalize this failure mode through a causal performative framework for recourse. We model how recourse actions propagate through a structural causal model, capturing interactions among features as well as their effect on the true label. These causal responses induce a non-convex optimization problem, even under standard convex losses. We characterize conditions under which performatively stable solutions exist and can be efficiently computed via simple iterative dynamics. Our analysis reveals that recourse policies that ignore causal structure can induce large, misaligned behavioral responses, whereas causal recourse leads to stable equilibria that reduce incentives for gaming. Experiments on both semi-synthetic and real credit datasets demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms standard empirical risk minimization while reducing the need for repeated model retraining to accommodate distribution shifts caused by strategic agent behavior.

</details>


### [112] [Same Graph Cross-Task Transfer in GNNs: Protocols and Predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28525)
*Neelam Akula,Surbhi Kumar,Murat Kantarcioglu,Baris Coskunuzer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了同图上节点分类（NC）与链接预测（LP）任务之间的迁移学习。通过提出无泄漏评估协议，发现NC→LP迁移在同配性图上普遍有益，而LP→NC迁移脆弱且可能产生负迁移；引入CoTask Score指标来评估共享编码器的联合效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界中许多图结构同时支持多种预测任务（如节点分类和链接预测），但现有评估方法因不兼容的数据划分、观测图假设和负采样规则，导致同图跨任务迁移的结论不可靠。需要建立规范的评估框架来研究任务间迁移的有效性和机制。

Method: 1) 提出无泄漏评估协议：固定节点和边的划分，使用排除评估边的共享消息传递图，为LP采用固定负样本；2) 在三种骨干网络（GCN、GraphSAGE、GPS）上进行系统实验；3) 引入CoTask Score (CTS) 量化共享编码器的联合NC+LP效用；4) 分析数据集统计特性（特别是同配性）对迁移效果的影响。

Result: 发现任务迁移具有强烈的方向性和可预测性：NC→LP在同配性图上普遍有益；LP→NC迁移脆弱，直接复用表征甚至可能降低准确率，仅在结构主导型场景下（LP简单但NC未饱和）才可靠地产生正向迁移；同配性等简单统计量可指导机制选择并避免负迁移。

Conclusion: 同图NC-LP任务迁移存在系统性规律：方向性强、可预测；应采用规范的评估协议避免数据泄漏；同配性等数据集特性可有效指导迁移策略选择；CoTask Score为共享编码器的多任务设计提供了实用评估工具。

Abstract: Many real-world graphs support multiple predictive tasks over the same underlying structure, creating an opportunity to reuse supervision across node classification (NC) and link prediction (LP). However, existing evaluations often rely on incompatible splits, observed-graph assumptions, and negative sampling rules, making conclusions about same-graph cross-task transfer unreliable. We formalize same-graph NC-LP transfer and propose a leakage-free protocol that fixes node and edge splits, uses a shared message-passing graph that excludes evaluated edges, and employs fixed negatives for LP. Across three backbones (GCN, GraphSAGE, GPS), we find that transfer is strongly directional and predictable: NC $\to$ LP is consistently beneficial on homophilic graphs, while LP $\to$ NC is fragile and can even degrade accuracy under naive representation reuse. LP $\to$ NC becomes reliably positive mainly in a structure-dominant regime where LP is easy but NC is unsaturated, suggesting that LP acts as structural pretraining. Finally, we introduce the CoTask Score (CTS) to summarize joint NC+LP utility when a shared encoder must serve both tasks, and show that simple dataset statistics, especially homophily, can guide mechanism choice and help avoid negative transfer.

</details>


### [113] [$β$-OPSD: Deriving with Policy Optimization, Training with Self-Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28582)
*Jiawei Xu,Minghui Liu,Juzheng Zhang,Tom Goldstein,Furong Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出β-OPSD方法，通过将原始OPSD算法视为β=1的特例，引入可调节的β参数控制KL惩罚权重，在数学推理基准测试中显著优于原始OPSD方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）方法在实践中表现脆弱，需要大量工程努力才能可靠工作。作者发现这种困难的根源在于：原始OPSD恰好是更广泛策略优化家族中β=1的成员，其中β权重是锚定学生模型到参考策略的KL惩罚项。

Method: 提出β-OPSD方法，推导出最优策略是参考策略与特权教师模型之间的几何插值。不直接使用强化学习优化目标（成本高、方差大），而是将闭式解转化为蒸馏目标：通过混合参考策略和教师模型的token级logits，每个β值选择参考-教师路径上的目标点，用廉价蒸馏近似昂贵策略优化的解。同时采用return-to-go信用分配方法，使token级更新与序列级目标对齐。

Result: 在数学推理基准测试上的实验表明，β-OPSD持续优于原始OPSD，改善了优化稳定性和下游推理性能。该方法提供了从自蒸馏到策略优化再返回的原则性路径，同时不牺牲OPSD的实用性效率。

Conclusion: β-OPSD成功地将β从隐式固定值转变为可控的正则化参数，在参考策略邻近性和特权教师指导之间进行权衡，为推理语言模型的改进提供了更稳定和有效的解决方案。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising approach to improve reasoning language models, but it remains brittle in practice: making it work reliably often requires substantial engineering effort. We identify a structural source of this difficulty: vanilla OPSD is precisely the $β=1$ member of a broader policy-optimization family, where $β$ weights the KL penalty anchoring the student to a reference policy. This equivalence turns $β$ from an implicit value fixed at one into a controllable regularization parameter, yielding a more general formulation that trades off proximity to a reference policy against privileged teacher guidance. We introduce $β$-OPSD and derive its optimal policy as a geometric interpolation between the reference policy and the privileged teacher. Directly optimizing this objective with reinforcement learning, however, would be costly and high-variance. Rather than optimize the RL objective directly, we turn its closed-form solution into a distillation target. Each value of $β$ selects a target along the reference-to-teacher path, which we implement efficiently by mixing their token-level logits. In this way, inexpensive distillation approximates the solution of expensive policy optimization. Return-to-go credit assignment further aligns token updates with the sequence-level objective while retaining the simplicity of OPSD. Experiments on mathematical reasoning benchmarks show that $β$-OPSD consistently outperforms vanilla OPSD, improving optimization stability and downstream reasoning performance. Our results provide a principled route from self-distillation to policy optimization and back without sacrificing the efficiency that makes OPSD practical.

</details>


### [114] [KAISEN: Reproducible Subgroup Fairness Auditing for Clinical Risk Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28608)
*Sparsh Roy,Samuel Girmachew,Nishita Chavan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了KAISEN，一个包含五个阶段的临床风险模型审计流程，并通过合成基准测试系统性地评估了各组件的可靠性，发现了四个关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床风险模型虽然在总体上表现良好，但在不同患者亚组间会产生显著不同的错误率。现有的审计流程很少进行压力测试，因此不清楚审计的哪些部分可以信任以及在什么条件下可信。

Method: 提出了KAISEN审计流程，包含亚组分层、差异测量、机制诊断、事后缓解和漂移监测五个阶段。在包含16种疾病任务、15个社会决定因素轴和3个预指定交集的合成基准上，对每个组件进行直至失败点的系统性评估。

Result: 发现了四个关键结果：(i) 显著性计数与标准化后的均等化几率差异(EOD)呈强相关(rho=0.78)；(ii) 分组阈值优化能持续减少EOD，而Platt缩放校准方法对EOD的影响接近随机；(iii) 机制诊断能正确识别所有受控案例，但在代理变量误指定情况下无法恢复任何模型驱动案例且无失败信号；(iv) CUSUM漂移监测的失败和误报更多与队列实现相关而非疾病本身，阈值难以跨队列迁移。

Conclusion: 审计流程的各个组件在特定条件下会出现可靠性问题，需要报告方差而非平均值。所有结果基于合成数据且具有已知真值，未建立临床有效性。研究提供了完整的代码、制品和复现脚本。

Abstract: Clinical risk models routinely achieve strong aggregate performance while producing materially different error rates across patient subgroups. Audit pipelines have been proposed to catch this, but their components are rarely stress-tested, so it is unclear which parts of an audit can be trusted and under what conditions. We present KAISEN, a five-phase audit pipeline covering subgroup stratification, disparity measurement, mechanism diagnostics, post-hoc mitigation, and drift monitoring, evaluated to the point of failure on a synthetic benchmark of 16 disease tasks, 15 social-determinant axes from Healthy People 2030, and three prespecified intersections. Four findings follow. (i) Significance tracks each axis's gap against its own minimum detectable effect: rank correlation between significance count and raw equalized-odds difference (EOD) across the 15 axes is rho = 0.56, rising to rho = 0.78 once EOD is standardized by that floor. (ii) Per-group threshold optimization reduces EOD in 48 of 48 held-out runs (paired delta = -0.285, 95% CI [-0.313, -0.252]), while group-wise Platt scaling -- the better calibrator -- behaves as a coin flip on EOD (19 of 48 runs improved, 95% CI [0.26, 0.55]) with mean effect near zero, so what an audit should report is the variance, not the average. (iii) The mechanism diagnostic classifies 144 of 144 controlled cases correctly but recovers none of 48 model-driven cases under proxy misspecification, with no signal that it failed. (iv) CUSUM failures and false alarms track cohort realization far more than disease: at the reference threshold, all 27 false alarms and 7 of 8 missed shifts come from different seeds (chi-squared p = 0.002), so a threshold tuned on one cohort fails to transfer. All results are synthetic with known ground truth and do not establish clinical validity. Code, artifacts, and scripts reproducing every number are released.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [O-RAN: Analysis of Latency-critical Interfaces and Overview of Time Sensitive Networking Solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27448)
*Esteban Municio,Gines Garcia-Aviles,Andres Garcia-Saavedra,Xavier Costa-Pérez*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文探讨了将时间敏感网络（TSN）标准应用于O-RAN架构，以在成本效益高的通用以太网上实现确定性性能，从而解决vRAN生态系统中的关键接口挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G/B5G/6G网络依赖于虚拟化无线接入网（vRAN），而O-RAN架构旨在通过开放接口和多厂商互操作组件实现灵活部署。然而，在成本效益高的共享传输网络（通用以太网）上保持确定性性能，特别是对于时间敏感接口，是一个重大挑战。传统专用链路成本高昂，需要新的解决方案。

Method: 分析了三种关键的TSN标准：IEEE 802.1CM、IEEE 802.1Qbu和IEEE 802.1Qbv，这些标准能够在成本效率网络上提供确定性保障。探讨了TSN使能的O-RAN架构设计空间，包括需求分析和部署选项，并评估了TSN技术与O-RAN集成的可行性。

Result: 确定了适用于O-RAN的TSN标准组合，提出了TSN使能的O-RAN架构设计方案，明确了部署需求和可选方案。研究揭示了TSN技术在O-RAN中应用的机会与挑战，为开放vRAN生态系统提供了技术路径。

Conclusion: TSN技术能够为O-RAN提供在成本效益高的通用以太网上实现确定性性能的解决方案，有助于实现vRAN的真正解耦和开放。然而，O-RAN在采用TSN技术时仍面临诸多挑战，需要进一步研究和标准化工作来完全开放vRAN生态系统。

Abstract: 5G and B5G/6G foundations heavily rely on virtualization technologies, and virtualized Radio Access Networks (vRANs) are one of their major keystones. However, while vRANs have been traditionally suffering from significant hardware/software coupling, next generation vRANs aim for open, standardized interfaces and multi-vendor, interoperable components to enable truly flexible deployments following the cloud-native principles. In this line, the O-RAN Alliance is promoting a novel Open RAN architecture to further boost flexibility and cost efficiency. In order to reduce costs and effectively achieve the promised disaggregation levels, O-RAN must ensure shared, integrated transport networks in opposition to dedicated, over-provisioned links from traditional approaches. However, keeping deterministic performance requirements in such cost-effective networks (i.e., general-purpose Ethernet networks), especially in those interfaces that are time-critical, is a challenge. In this article, we review the most relevant Time Sensitive Networking (TSN) standards that may bring compelling benefits to O-RAN (i.e., IEEE 802.1CM, IEEE 802.1Qbu and IEEE 802.1Qbv) for providing determinism over cost-efficient networks. We explore the design space for a TSN-enabled O-RAN architecture, reporting on the requirements and deployment options and finally, we discuss on the opportunities and challenges that O-RAN will face when adopting TSN technologies to fully open the vRAN ecosystem.

</details>


### [116] [Localization and Pursuit of a Mobile Target using Distance-only Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27812)
*Nabarupa Das,Suvadip Batabyal*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种仅利用距离测量在二维平面中对线性运动目标进行定位和追踪的方法，证明了最多13步即可完成目标定位和运动向量确定，并能保持追踪误差有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的目标定位和追踪系统通常依赖GPS、角度传感或多锚节点，这些要求增加了系统复杂性和成本。本文旨在开发一种仅使用距离测量（基于路径损耗估计）的简化系统，无需上述依赖即可实现对线性运动目标的定位和追踪。

Method: 系统由位于原点的接收器和一个移动智能体组成。方法分为两阶段：首先识别目标所在象限并估计其位置，然后利用连续的位置估计确定目标的运动向量，并据此更新智能体的追踪轨迹。整个过程中仅使用距离测量信息，无需角度或其他辅助信息。

Result: 理论证明最多需要13步即可完成目标定位及其运动向量的确定。仿真结果表明，智能体能够成功从搜索阶段过渡到追踪阶段，并且在追踪过程中能够保持追踪误差有界。

Conclusion: 本文提出的仅基于距离测量的定位和追踪方法是可行且有效的，能够在有限步骤内完成目标定位，并实现稳定的目标追踪，为简化目标追踪系统提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates localization and pursuit of a linearly moving target in a two-dimensional plane using distance measurements only. The distance from the target is estimated from pathloss measurements and is assumed to be noise-free. The proposed system consists of a receiver that is stationed at the origin and a mobile agent. The system does not rely on GPS, angle sensing, or multiple anchor nodes to localize the target. The proposed method first identifies the target quadrant and estimates its position. Consecutive target position estimates are then used to determine the motion vector and update the agent trajectory during pursuit. We show that a maximum of thirteen steps are required to localize the target and determine its motion vector. Simulation results show that the agent can successfully make a transition from search to pursuit phase, and also maintain a bounded tracking error.

</details>


### [117] [Geometric View on Integrated Cascaded Channel of IRS-Aided Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27972)
*Yunli Li,Young Jin Chun*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对混合智能反射面（IRS）部署问题，提出了基于几何模型的全局最优部署策略和机会关联策略，显著优于传统最近关联策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IRS研究普遍假设IRS位于收发器之间且高度差远小于链路距离，该假设虽简化分析却导致次优性能，缺乏全局最优部署策略。

Method: 采用几何模型（乘积距离路径损耗法则对应Cassini卵形线、和距离路径损耗法则对应椭圆）确定混合IRS最优位置，并设计基于集成路径损耗模型的机会关联策略。

Result: 仿真验证表明，所提方法显著优于传统最近关联策略，尤其对混合IRS和主动IRS效果更佳。

Conclusion: 提出的几何模型方法和机会关联策略实现了IRS部署的全局优化，突破了现有部分选择策略的性能限制。

Abstract: The hybrid intelligent reflecting surface (IRS) architecture is a novel technology that leverages the advantages of both passive and active IRS; the passive IRS offers a large aperture, while the active IRS provides additional power amplification. Prior studies have shown that the optimal performance of IRS-assisted wireless networks is achieved when the passive IRS is deployed near the transceivers and the active IRS is closer to the receiver with decreasing amplification power, assuming that the IRS is located between transceivers and that the height difference between IRS and transceivers is much smaller than the link distance between transceivers. However, most of the prior works on IRS blindly adopted this assumption in the IRS association policy, which essentially becomes a partial selection strategy that offers analytical simplicity at the cost of sub-optimal performance. This limitation motivated us to find the globally optimal deployment strategy for all types of IRS. To this end, we first employ the \emph{geometric models} for integrated path loss distance (known as Cassini oval and Ellipse for product- and sum-distance path loss laws, respectively) and use them to determine the optimal locations of the hybrid IRS. Then, we design a novel \emph{opportunistic association policy} for hybrid IRS based on the integrated path loss model. Furthermore, we validate our proposed methods through simulations and show that they significantly outperform the conventional nearest association policy, especially for hybrid and active IRS.

</details>


### [118] [Layered Architecture for Mobile Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28083)
*Qingwen Liu,Mingqing Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了移动AI栈（Mobile AI Stack）概念，这是一个移动感知的架构框架，整合了移动能源网络、高效AI芯片、云边端基础设施、分布式AI模型和具身AI应用五个紧密耦合的层级，为大规模移动智能系统提供了系统性视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI框架主要假设静态的、以云为中心的计算范式，然而自动驾驶、无人机、机器人和可穿戴系统等新兴智能应用需要在高度动态和移动的环境中运行。这种转变将移动性作为整个AI生态系统的基本约束，影响能源供应、计算和智能部署。

Method: 引入移动AI栈（Mobile AI Stack）概念，这是一个移动感知的架构框架，整合五个紧密耦合的层级：移动能源网络、高效AI芯片、云边端基础设施、分布式AI模型和具身AI应用，系统性地研究能源输送、计算架构、通信网络和AI算法如何协同演进。

Result: 提出了一个概念性框架，提供了理解能源输送、计算架构、通信网络和AI算法如何协同演进的系统性视角，为构建可扩展、可靠和能效高的移动AI系统奠定了理论基础。

Conclusion: 移动AI栈为下一代基础设施提供了概念蓝图，该基础设施深度融合了移动智能的计算、能源和通信网络，为未来大规模移动智能系统的发展指明了方向。

Abstract: Artificial intelligence (AI) is rapidly evolving from a centralized computing capability into a pervasive infrastructure that interacts directly with the physical world. While recent perspectives highlight the roles of energy, chips, infrastructure, models, and applications in enabling large-scale AI systems, these frameworks primarily assume a static, cloud-centric computing paradigm. However, emerging intelligent applications, including autonomous vehicles, drones, robots, and wearable systems, require AI to operate in highly dynamic and mobile environments. This shift introduces mobility as a fundamental constraint across the entire AI ecosystem, affecting energy supply, computation, and intelligence deployment. In this article, we introduce the concept of the Mobile AI Stack, a mobility-aware architectural framework that integrates five tightly coupled layers: mobile energy networks, energy-efficient AI chips, cloud-edge-mobile infrastructure, distributed AI models, and embodied AI applications. The proposed framework provides a systematic perspective for understanding how energy delivery, computing architectures, communication networks, and AI algorithms must co-evolve to support large-scale mobile intelligence. We further discuss key research challenges and future directions toward building scalable, reliable, and energy-efficient mobile AI systems. Mobile AI Stack offers a conceptual blueprint of the next-generation infrastructure which deeply integrates the networks of computation, energy, and communications for mobile intelligence.

</details>


### [119] [PCAP-LM: An LLM-Native Text Representation for TLS Bulk Traffic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28100)
*Xavier Marjou,Lucas Tamic,Ilan Jaffeux-Cheniout*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了PCAP-LM，一种面向流量的LLM原生文本表示方法，通过PacketGlyphs字母表和优化的tokenizer，将网络数据包捕获文件压缩812倍，使LLM在流量分析任务中的准确率从51.0%提升至99.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在网络流量分析方面具有强大的推理能力，但标准的数据包捕获格式及其文本表示过于冗长，超出LLM上下文窗口两个数量级，导致无法直接应用于完整的网络流量分析任务。

Method: 提出PCAP-LM框架，包括：(1) PacketGlyphs - 新颖的ASCII字母表，编码数据包方向、TCP/TLS状态、对数尺度大小和包间延迟；(2) 受约束的PMI-BPE分词器；(3) 模式行程编码，有效压缩重复行为模式；(4) @REFS侧索引保留无损的原数据包访问能力。将原始捕获文件转码为语义摘要，实现有损知识提取。

Result: 在5G/4G TLS 1.3批量下载流量数据集上，BPE词表在159个token时完全饱和，相比tshark -V实现812倍的大小缩减。在30个留出文件的取证问答评估中，前沿LLM在PCAP-LM文档上达到99.3%准确率，而在token预算匹配的tshark -V前缀上仅51.0%。但有损设计引入了已知盲点，如TCP重传24%的假阴性率。

Conclusion: PCAP-LM成功解决了网络流量捕获文件超出LLM上下文窗口的问题，通过有损转码实现了大幅压缩并显著提升了LLM分析准确率，但需要权衡信息损失和扩展到异构混合协议环境的挑战。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) offer powerful reasoning capabilities for network traffic analysis, but standard capture formats and their textual equivalents are prohibitively verbose, overflowing LLM context windows by two orders of magnitude. We present PCAP-LM, a flow-centric, LLM-native text representation that acts as a lossy knowledge extraction step rather than a standard compression tool: raw captures are transcoded into semantic summaries using PacketGlyphs - a novel ASCII alphabet coined in this paper that encodes packet direction, TCP/TLS state, log-scale size, and inter-packet delay. Combined with a constrained PMI-BPE tokenizer and motif run-length encoding, repetitive behavioural patterns are aggressively collapsed. A @REFS side-index preserves lossless drill-down into the original packets. Evaluated on a homogeneous corpus of 5G/4G TLS 1.3 bulk-download traffic, the BPE vocabulary fully saturates at 159 tokens, achieving an 812x size reduction over tshark -V and fitting entire captures within a single LLM context window. In a forensic question-answering evaluation over 30 held-out files, a frontier LLM achieves 99.3% accuracy from PCAP-LM documents versus 51.0% from a token-budget-matched tshark -V prefix. The lossy design introduces known blind spots - most notably a 24% false-negative rate for TCP retransmissions - and extending to heterogeneous mixed-protocol environments will require vocabulary retraining.

</details>


### [120] [Powering Net-Zero 6G: Packetized Energy Management for Grid-Interactive Telecom Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28111)
*Adnan Aijaz,Xinyi Lin*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"分组化能源管理（PEM）"的新型框架，旨在将6G无线接入网（RAN）基础设施转化为具有能源意识和电网互动能力的资产，通过将灵活需求建模为可调度的能源数据包，实现峰值感知运营、可再生能源利用率提升以及中断情况下的服务连续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着未来6G网络集成原生AI操作、边缘智能、密集部署、可再生能源和储能系统，RAN将成为不断增长的电力消费者和分布式能源灵活性的来源。传统的RAN效率机制已不足以应对向净零排放6G转型的挑战，因此需要超越传统机制的新型能源管理方法。

Method: 提出分组化能源管理（PEM）框架，将灵活需求表示为可调度的能源数据包，这些数据包可根据本地约束、可再生能源可用性、碳强度、电价和通信优先级进行准入、延迟或重构。研究构建了启用PEM的基站模型、支持PEM集成的RAN架构，以及用于聚合PEM站点的电信虚拟电厂（VPP）概念。

Result: 仿真结果证明了PEM在峰值感知运营、提高可再生能源利用率以及实现中断弹性服务连续性方面的潜力。

Conclusion: PEM框架成功将6G基础设施转化为能源感知、电网互动的资产，为电信与能源系统的协同设计提供了新的思路。文章还讨论了电信-能源协同设计面临的开放性挑战，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The transition to net-zero 6G requires energy-management approaches that go beyond conventional RAN efficiency mechanisms. As future networks integrate AI-native operation, edge intelligence, dense deployments, renewables, and storage, the RAN will become both a growing power consumer and a source of distributed energy flexibility. This paper introduces packetized energy management (PEM) as a framework for transforming 6G infrastructure into energy-aware, grid-interactive assets. PEM represents flexible demand as schedulable energy packets that can be admitted, deferred, or reshaped according to local constraints, renewable availability, carbon intensity, price, and communication priorities. We present a PEM-enabled base-station model, a RAN architecture for PEM integration, and the telecoms virtual power plant (VPP) concept for aggregating PEM-enabled sites. Simulation results demonstrate PEM's potential for peak-aware operation, improved renewable utilization, and outage-resilient service continuity. The paper also discusses open challenges for telco-energy co-design.

</details>


### [121] [Coexistence of 5G NR and Wi Fi 6E/7 at 6 GHz: Experimental Interference Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28213)
*Rafik Zitouni,Demos Serghiou,Ali Dagdeviren,Tajinder Randhawa,Edwards Udean,Hanli Dong,Riccardo Pozza,Rahim Tafazolli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 首次在商用VLP Wi-Fi 6E/7设备与真实5G NR系统的传导干扰测量表明，合规VLP设备会在造成干扰前通过LBT机制退避信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6 GHz频段（band n102）被同时分配给5G NR和Wi-Fi 6E/7系统使用，存在潜在干扰风险。现有研究多基于仿真或辐射测试，缺乏真实商用VLP Wi-Fi设备对完整5G系统的实际干扰测量数据，因此需要实证研究验证VLP设备对5G系统的干扰影响及合规性。

Method: 构建基于OpenAirInterface的SDR测试平台，支持band n102（40 MHz带宽，30 kHz子载波间隔），包含完整O-RAN/5G核心网栈。在屏蔽环境中进行传导干扰测量，将商用VLP Wi-Fi 6E/7设备注入信号分别耦合至gNB上行和UE下行接收链路，扫描注入功率并记录吞吐量、误块率、信噪比等性能指标。

Result: 接收功率低于-75 dBm时，gNB和UE均无可测量的性能下降；超过此阈值后性能逐渐恶化。低数据速率下UE抗干扰能力更强，仅发送beacon帧时UE不受影响。链路预算分析将测量阈值映射为等效距离，这些距离均落在LBT的545-685米排除区内。

Conclusion: 合规VLP设备会在其发射功率达到可能损害5G接收机的水平之前，通过Listen Before Talk机制检测5G信号并退避信道，证明VLP Wi-Fi与5G NR在6 GHz频段共存是安全的。

Abstract: This paper presents the first conducted-interference measurements of a commercial Very Low Power (VLP) Wi-Fi 6E/7 device into both the gNB uplink and UE downlink receiver chains of a live 5G New Radio (NR) system, using a complete O-RAN/SDR stack with 5G core in band~n102 (6\,GHz). We use a Software-Defined Radio (SDR) testbed built on OpenAirInterface with band~n102 support (40 MHz, 30 kHz Subcarrier Spacing). We sweep the injected Wi-Fi power and record throughput, block error rate, and signal-to-noise ratio on both the gNB uplink and UE downlink paths. Neither receiver shows measurable degradation below 75 dBm. Above this threshold, performance degrades progressively. The UE is more resilient at lower data rates and unaffected by beacon-only transmissions. A complementary link-budget analysis maps these measured thresholds to equivalent VLP-to-victim distances. These distances fall well inside the 545--685 Listen Before Talk (LBT) exclusion zone, confirming that a compliant VLP device would vacate the channel before its emissions could harm either receiver.

</details>


### [122] [Observing the Relationship between QoS Unpredictability, Prediction Error, and User Activity in a Remote Desktop Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28216)
*Keisuke Ishibashi,Xuliang Deng,Yoshiaki Kitaguchi,Kenichi Nagami,Ichiro Mizukoshi,Akira Sato,Daiyu Nobori*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本研究利用真实RDS使用日志，发现RTT的平均值、时序波动和瞬时偏差均与用户活动显著相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于远程桌面服务（RDS）中QoS指标与用户活动关系的研究多在实验环境下进行，样本有限且难以反映真实使用场景，导致QoS时序波动与用户活动的关系未被充分探索。

Method: 基于Thin-Telework System的真实使用日志，分析往返时间（RTT）、发送包数量和每用户接收字节数的时间序列数据，研究QoS统计特征与用户活动之间的关系。

Result: 不仅RTT平均值，其时序波动（如随时间的标准差）和瞬时偏差（相对于均值的瞬时偏离）也与用户活动显著相关。这些特征分别对应用户视角的QoS不可预测性和预测误差。

Conclusion: QoS的波动特征（不可预测性和预测误差）对用户行为有重要影响，可能揭示了用户行为背后的心理机制，为提升远程桌面服务质量提供了新视角。

Abstract: With the increasing need for remote work, especially since the COVID-19 era, Remote Desktop Services (RDS) have become widely used. Because interactive RDS usage depends heavily on communication quality, some studies have investigated the relationship between QoS metrics and user activity in RDS. However, these works have een conducted in experimental environments, where the number of samples is limited and may not reflect real-world usage. Consequently, the relationship between temporal fluctuations in QoS and user activity remains underexplored. This paper investigates the relationship between QoS statistics and user activity using real-world usage logs of an RDS, Thin-Telework System. We analyze time-series data of round-trip time (RTT), the number of sent packets, and the number of received bytes per user. Notably, we find that not only the average RTT but also its temporal fluctuation (e.g., standard deviation over time) and its instantaneous deviation from the mean are significantly associated with user activity. From the users' perspective, these features correspond to QoS unpredictability and the prediction error, respectively, and may provide insights into psychological mechanisms underlying user behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.AR'></div>

# cs.AR [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Analog Courant Numbers and their Role in Analog Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27609)
*Arash Ghasemi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文发现了模拟计算矩阵求解电路中归一化最快模式的一个上限约束，该约束由电路行的组合单位增益带宽决定，类似于时间步进方法中的Courant数限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模拟计算中，矩阵行通常由阻抗网络表示，但缺乏对其物理运算速度限制的理论约束。本文旨在揭示这种硬件在表示和解析算子速率方面的固有界限。

Method: 通过理论推导确定了归一化最快模式的上限为电路各行中最大组合单位增益带宽的$2π$倍。组合单位增益带宽由该行的有限增益调整单位增益带宽以及耦合行的贡献构成。使用大规模LTspice仿真验证该理论，涵盖CMOS至热电子真空管等多种电路架构，并在热传导方程、基于图的半监督学习和图正则化回归等基准任务上测试。

Result: 理论推导得到了归一化最快模式的显式上界公式，仿真结果在多种电路架构和应用场景中验证了该约束的有效性。

Conclusion: 该约束类似于时间步进方法中的Courant数限制，从根本上限制了模拟硬件能够物理表示并在输出端解析的算子速率，为模拟计算系统的设计与应用提供了重要理论指导。

Abstract: This paper identifies a dynamical constraint on analog-computing approaches in which a row of the matrix is represented by an impedance network. It shows that the fastest normalized mode is no more than $2π$ times the largest combined unity-gain bandwidth (CUGBW) among all the circuit rows. The CUGBW of a row equals its finite-gain-adjusted unity-gain bandwidth plus the contributions of all rows coupled to it. Each contribution is the square root of the product of the two rows' unity-gain bandwidths multiplied by their coupling conductance and divided by the square root of the product of their total conductance loadings. This bound plays a role analogous to the Courant-number restriction in time-stepping methods by limiting the operator rates that analog hardware can physically represent and resolve at its outputs. The theory is validated using large-scale LTspice simulations across architectures ranging from CMOS to thermionic vacuum-tube circuits. The benchmark circuits implement a one-dimensional heat equation, a graph-based semi-supervised learning problem, and a graph-regularized regression.

</details>


### [124] [GyRot: Leveraging Hidden Synergy between Rotation and Fine-grained Group Quantization for Low-bit LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27694)
*Sangjin Kim,Yuseon Choi,Byeongcheol Kim,Jungjun Oh,Hoi-jun Yoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出GyRot框架，通过软硬件协同设计解决了旋转量化与分组量化结合时的精度损失和硬件开销问题，在INT4架构上实现了LLaMA模型系列的SOTA 4-bit精度，并带来最高3.4x加速和3.6x能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特量化对大语言模型的高效推理至关重要，旋转量化和细粒度分组量化各自表现出色，但两者结合时往往因旋转的全局性与分组缩放的局部性不匹配而导致精度下降或硬件开销增加。

Method: 提出GyRot量化框架和硬件加速器：1) 引入Coarse Rotation, Fine Grouping (CoRFiG)和Harmonic-Aligned Permutation (HAP)实现旋转与分组量化的协作集成；2) 重新表述非对称量化并提出零点舍入策略以实现全整数量化；3) 基于INT4张量处理单元架构实现。

Result: 在LLaMA系列模型上实现了业界最佳的4-bit量化精度；相比基线LLM加速器实现最高3.4倍加速和3.6倍能效提升。

Conclusion: GyRot通过算法-硬件协同设计成功解决了旋转与分组量化结合的关键挑战，验证了其在可扩展和节能的LLM部署中的实际有效性。

Abstract: Low-bit quantization is essential for efficient LLM inference, and both rotation and fine-grained group quantization have shown individual promise. However, their combination often leads to accuracy degradation or hardware overhead due to a mismatch between the global nature of rotation and the localized behavior of group scaling. We propose GyRot, a quantization framework and hardware accelerator that bridges this gap through algorithm-hardware co-design. GyRot introduces Coarse Rotation, Fine Grouping (CoRFiG) and Harmonic-Aligned Permutation (HAP) to enable cooperative integration of rotation and group quantization, enhancing quantizability while relaxing scaling factor precision. To further reduce hardware cost, we reformulate asymmetric quantization and introduce a zero-point rounding strategy that enables fully integer dequantization. Implemented on an INT4-based tensor PE architecture, GyRot achieves state-of-the-art 4-bit accuracy across LLaMA-family models, while delivering up to 3.4x speedup and 3.6x energy efficiency over baseline LLM accelerators. These results validate GyRot's practical effectiveness for scalable and energy-efficient LLM deployment.

</details>


### [125] [LightRot: A Light-Weighted Rotation Scheme and Architecture for Accurate Low-Bit Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27704)
*Sangjin Kim,Yuseon Choi,Jungjun Oh,Byeongcheol Kim,Hoi-Jun Yoo*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了LightRot，一种面向低比特LLM推理的轻量级旋转方案和专用硬件加速器，在28nm工艺下实现了27.4 TOPS/W的能效，超越现有设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在各领域展现卓越能力，如何在保持推理精度的同时实现能效优化成为关键挑战。传统低比特量化方法存在旋转操作能耗高、精度损失等问题，且现有工作多基于简单模型评估，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 提出Grouped Local Rotation（GLR）和Outlier Direction Aligning（ODA）算法，结合分层快速哈达玛变换（FHT）旋转单元，设计专用硬件加速器架构。该方法有效解决了低比特量化中的旋转能耗问题，并针对LLaMA2-13B、LLaMA3-8B等先进模型进行优化。

Result: 在28nm CMOS工艺下实现，4-bit推理峰值能效达27.4 TOPS/W，超越现有最优设计。在MT-Bench真实对话场景验证中表现稳健，证明了对聊天式AI系统的实用性。

Conclusion: LightRot通过算法创新与硬件效率的协同优化，为可扩展的低比特LLM推理建立了新范式，为可持续AI发展铺平道路，重新定义了聊天式AI系统的基准。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) continue to demonstrate exceptional capabilities across various domains, the challenge of achieving energy-efficient and accurate inference becomes increasingly critical. This work presents LightRot, a lightweight rotation scheme and dedicated hardware accelerator designed for low-bit LLM inference. The proposed architecture integrates Grouped Local Rotation (GLR) and Outlier Direction Aligning (ODA) algorithms with a hierarchical Fast Hadamard Transform (FHT)-based rotation unit to address key challenges in low-bit quantization, including the energy overhead of rotation operations. The proposed accelerator, implemented in a 28nm CMOS process, achieves a peak energy efficiency of 27.4 TOPS/W for 4-bit inference, surpassing prior state-of-the-art designs. Unlike conventional approaches that rely on higher-precision inference or evaluate on basic language modeling tasks like GPT-2, LightRot is optimized for advanced models such as LLaMA2-13B and LLaMA3-8B. Its performance is further validated on MT-Bench, demonstrating robust applicability to real-world conversational scenarios and redefining benchmarks for chat-based AI systems. By synergizing algorithmic innovations and hardware efficiency, this work sets a new paradigm for scalable, low-bit LLM inference, paving the way for sustainable AI advancements.

</details>


### [126] [ARES: Adaptive Reasoning-Effort Steering for PPA- and Cost-Aware RTL Optimization with LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27879)
*Stef Cuyckens,Mihaela Jivanescu,Jun Yin,Chao Fang,Marian Verhelst*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出了Ares框架，通过标准化成本度量、简化记忆机制和动态推理策略，在RTL设计优化中以更低成本实现更优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM代理在优化RTL设计时存在三个问题：未报告标准化成本、过度归功于工程化的跨设计记忆、每次调用保持固定推理成本，导致无法公平比较不同优化器的效率。

Method: 提出Ares框架包含三项创新：（1）引入每LLM调用的标准化美元成本作为评价指标；（2）发现工程化长期记忆相比简单拼接无明显优势；（3）通过耐心计数器自适应调整每次调用的推理深度，仅在低推理层次进展停滞时才升级到更深推理。

Result: 在三个测试设计上，自适应推理策略在相同标准化成本下降低FoM指标23-27%，而最佳固定推理仅达16-23%；Ares以12%的token成本达到比Dr. RTL优25%的FoM，并缩小83%的LLM起草与手工优化MAC单元之间的差距。

Conclusion: 通过标准化成本度量和自适应推理分配，Ares证明了推理资源的智能分配比记忆工程更关键，为LLM辅助RTL设计优化提供了更高效且成本可控的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents optimize the power, performance, and area (PPA) of register-transfer-level (RTL) designs by iterating over edits, synthesis, and PPA analysis, paying a dollar cost for every LLM call. Prior agents report the quality reached without its normalized cost, attribute that quality to an engineered cross-design memory, and hold the reasoning effort of every call fixed. We propose Ares with three corresponding innovations. (1) We introduce a normalized dollar cost per LLM call reported alongside the figure of merit (FoM), enabling fair comparison across effort levels and optimizers. (2) Using this accounting, we find the construction of the long-term memory matters little. An engineered memory brings no dependable gain over a plain concatenation of the same experience. (3) We instead adapt the per-call reasoning effort by escalating to deeper reasoning only once progress at a lower effort stalls, via a patience counter fit on 21 training designs, allocating reasoning where it pays rather than uniformly across all iterations. On three test designs unseen during training, the effort policy lowers the FoM by 23-27% where the best fixed effort reaches 16-23%, at equal normalized cost. Ares closes up to 83% of the gap from an LLM-drafted multiply-accumulate unit to its highly hand-optimized counterpart, and reaches a 25% deeper FoM than state-of-the-art Dr. RTL at 12% of its tokens.

</details>


### [127] [Demystifying DRAM Read Disturbance: Bridging the Gap Between Experimental Characterization and Device-Level Modeling of RowHammer and RowPress Phenomena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28233)
*Haocong Luo,Longda Zhou,Ataberk Olgun,İsmail Emir Yüksel,Nisa Bostanci,Zhigang Ji,Xing Wu,Onur Mutlu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文通过TCAD仿真弥合了DRAM读干扰（RowHammer和RowPress）的实验特性与器件级物理机制之间的差距，揭示了现有模型无法解释的现象，并提出了更新的错误机制和关键建模参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作在实验特性和器件级物理机制之间存在差距和不一致性，物理机制无法完全解释主要实验观察结果，缺乏理解、表征和缓解DRAM读干扰的原则性基础。

Method: 识别并演示现有器件级模型与实验特性之间的差距，聚焦三个基本指标（位翻转方向、位翻转计数、最小攻击行激活次数）；开展全面严格的TCAD仿真，验证与实验观察现象的匹配性。

Result: 总结了对理解RowHammer和RowPress位翻转的更新器件级错误机制；识别了显著影响仿真结果与实际芯片特性匹配度的关键建模和仿真参数。

Conclusion: 为未来DRAM读干扰研究提供了原则性基础，对严谨全面的实验特性表征方法和读干扰缓解技术的设计具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: DRAM read disturbance, like RowHammer and RowPress, is a critical robustness issue where accessing DRAM can cause unintended bitflips in other unaccessed DRAM locations. DRAM read disturbance bitflips significantly impact the safe, secure, and reliable operation of DRAM-based computing systems. Many prior works experimentally characterize these bitflips and propose mitigations based on empirical results. Other device-level works study their underlying physical mechanisms, but these mechanisms do not fully explain all major empirical observations.
  Our goal is to bridge the gap between experimental characterization and device-level modeling and understanding of RowHammer and RowPress, providing a principled foundation for future work on understanding, characterizing, and mitigating DRAM read disturbance. We first identify and demonstrate gaps and inconsistencies between the physical mechanisms of RowHammer and RowPress described by existing device-level models and experimental characterization of their bitflips. We focus on three fundamental metrics that should map to first-order physical mechanisms: 1) bitflip directions, 2) bitflip counts, and 3) the minimum number of aggressor row activations that trigger the first bitflips (i.e., ACmin). Second, we present a comprehensive and rigorous set of TCAD simulations that match phenomena observed in experimental characterizations of RowHammer and RowPress bitflips.
  From our results, we 1) summarize updated device-level error mechanisms for understanding RowHammer and RowPress bitflips, and 2) identify key modeling and simulation parameters that significantly affect whether simulation results match real-chip characterization. We discuss implications for 1) rigorous, comprehensive, and efficient experimental characterization methodologies of DRAM read disturbance bitflips, and 2) the design of DRAM read disturbance mitigation techniques.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [ESBT: A Scalable and Deterministic Sequence CRDT for Distributed Collaborative Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28101)
*Moulay Driss Mechaoui,Abdessamad Imine*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了扩展Stern-Brocot树（ESBT），一种用于分布式协同文本编辑的标识符分配方案，有效解决了现有序列CRDT在长期高并发编辑会话中标识符增长失控和内存消耗过高的问题，显著提升了系统响应速度并降低了内存占用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列CRDT虽然保证了强最终一致性（SEC），但在长期运行的高并发编辑会话中存在标识符增长失控和内存消耗持续增加的问题，严重限制了其可扩展性和性能，亟需一种能够控制标识符增长同时保持确定性和一致性的解决方案。

Method: 提出扩展Stern-Brocot树（ESBT）标识符分配方案，构建一个密集、确定且紧凑的标识符空间。该分配策略在保持确定性排序和强最终一致性的同时，有效限制了标识符的增长。

Result: 在多达100,000个并发操作、50个协作站点的实验中，ESBT在纯插入负载下响应速度提升28-88%，在混合插入/删除负载下提升59-74%，标识符内存消耗降低50-75%。在对抗性中间插入负载（10,000次操作）下，响应速度提升86.53%，标识符大小减少92.81%。

Conclusion: ESBT有效解决了现有序列CRDT的主要可扩展性限制，为大规模协同编辑系统提供了高效且可扩展的基础，证明了数学方法在优化分布式系统性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: Modern collaborative editing systems require efficient mechanisms for managing concurrent updates across distributed replicas. Sequence Conflict-free Replicated Data Types (CRDTs) have become the de facto standard for supporting decentralized collaboration; they enable decentralized replicas to apply operations in arbitrary order while converging to a common state. Although existing sequence CRDTs guarantee Strong Eventual Consistency (SEC), they often suffer from uncontrolled identifier growth and increasing memory consumption during long-running, highly concurrent editing sessions, which severely limits their scalability and performance. This paper presents the Extended Stern-Brocot Tree (ESBT), a mathematically grounded identifier allocation scheme for distributed collaborative text editing, providing a dense, deterministic, and compact identifier space. The proposed allocation strategy bounds identifier growth while preserving deterministic ordering and Strong Eventual Consistency. Experimental evaluation using workloads of up to 100,000 concurrent operations generated across 50 collaborating sites shows that ESBT improves responsiveness by 28 to 88% under pure insertions and 59 to 74% under mixed insertion/deletion workloads, while reducing identifier memory consumption by 50 to 75% in beginning and random insertion patterns compared with the best-performing baseline sequence CRDTs (Logoot and LSEQ). Under the adversarial middle-insertion workload (10,000 operations), ESBT further improves responsiveness by 86.53% and reduces identifier size by 92.81%. These results demonstrate that ESBT effectively addresses the principal scalability limitations of existing sequence CRDTs and provides an efficient and scalable foundation for large-scale collaborative editing systems.

</details>


### [129] [SmartGen: Seamless Disaggregated LLM Inference with Selective KV Cache Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28150)
*Xuchuan Luo,Jiacheng Shen,Xin Wang,Yangfan Zhou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出SmartGen，一种选择性KV缓存传输引擎，通过三种数据传输路径缓解分离式LLM推理中的网络瓶颈，将TTST降低多达4.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前分离式LLM推理架构在自托管云实例部署中面临严峻挑战：预填充节点与解码节点之间需要传输大量KV缓存，容易耗尽有限的节点间网络带宽，成为系统瓶颈。

Method: SmartGen设计三条数据传输路径：(1)基于性能分析的前瞻性传输路径，在预填充阶段识别并推送关键KV缓存条目至解码节点；(2)并行按需传输路径，在解码阶段同步获取远程与本地KV缓存；(3)推测性传输路径，最终将全部KV缓存投递至解码节点。

Result: 实验结果表明，与典型的全量KV缓存传输方法相比，SmartGen将第二令牌时间（TTST）降低多达4.3倍，同时保持相当的后续解码性能和准确率。

Conclusion: SmartGen有效缓解了分离式LLM推理的网络瓶颈问题，显著降低了推理延迟，为自托管云环境下的LLM部署提供了一种高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Disaggregating the prefill and decoding stages of large language model (LLM) inference into two separate sets of nodes is widely adopted in today's LLM serving systems. However, such an architecture poses significant challenges for self-hosted LLM deployments on rented cloud instances, since transferring enormous key-value (KV) caches between disaggregated nodes can easily saturate the limited inter-node network bandwidth. In this paper, we propose to mitigate the network bottleneck by selectively transferring essential KV cache entries across the two stages. There are two challenges to achieve selective KV cache transfer, i.e., accurate KV selection during the prefill stage, and efficient KV fetching during the decoding stage. To address these challenges, we design SmartGen, a KV cache transfer engine that allows seamless disaggregated LLM inference with three data transfer paths. Specifically, we leverage 1) a profile-based proactive transfer path to identify and push essential KV cache entries to the decoding node during the prefill stage, 2) a parallel on-demand transfer path to simultaneously fetch remote and local KV cache entries during the decoding stage, and 3) a speculative transfer path to finally deliver all KV caches to the decoding node. Experimental results show that SmartGen reduces time-to-second-token by up to 4.3x compared with the typical full KV cache transfer approach while offering comparable subsequent decoding performance and accuracy.

</details>


### [130] [A Cloud Continuum Research Infrastructure for Distributed CPS Experimentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28193)
*Fabio Orazio Mirto,Giuseppe Tricomi,Luca D'Agati,Andrea Sabbioni,Stefano Silvestri,Francesco Longo,Giovanni Merlino,Armir Bujari,Paolo Bellavista,Antonio Puliafito*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于SLICES蓝图的双层参考架构，用于Cloud Continuum实验环境，支持异构资源的组合并保证实验的可重复性、可观测性和可控性。通过能源社区管理和AirWatch两个用例验证了架构的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cloud Continuum应用需要能够组合异构的边缘、雾、云和高性能计算资源的实验环境，同时保持分布式部署的可重复性、可观测性和可控性。现有环境缺乏统一的架构来支持多领域应用在分布式基础设施上的灵活部署和对比实验。

Method: 提出双层参考架构：1）研究基础设施层，负责暴露和管理分布式资源；2）应用层，按照Edge-Fog-Cloud模式组织信息物理工作流，将放置、时序和数据溯源作为一等实验要素。Edge层处理物理设备交互和低延迟感知，Fog层执行近源协调和流处理，Cloud层进行全局分析优化。支持在相同可编程基础设施上部署和比较不同的控制监控策略。

Result: 通过两个代表性用例验证：1）可再生能源社区管理，需要分布式数字孪生协调和基于时间窗口的能量控制；2）AirWatch监控流水线，专注于异常检测、低延迟告警和云端聚合。在地理分布式基础设施上进行了40次系统实验运行，比较了虚拟化和物理边缘部署的性能表现。

Conclusion: 所提架构成功支持多种continuum应用而非单一领域原型，使研究人员能够在相同的可编程基础设施基板上部署、定制和比较不同的控制和监控策略，为Cloud Continuum实验提供了有效的环境支撑。

Abstract: Cloud Continuum applications require experimental environments capable of combining heterogeneous Edge, Fog, Cloud, and high-performance computing resources while preserving reproducibility, observability, and control over distributed deployments. This paper presents a two-level reference architecture for Cloud Continuum experimentation built on top of the SLICES Cloud Continuum Blueprint. The proposed approach separates the research-infrastructure layer, which exposes and manages distributed resources, from the application layer, where Cyber-Physical workflows are organized according to an Edge-Fog-Cloud pattern in which placement, timing, and data provenance are treated as first-class experimental concerns.
  The architecture is designed to support multiple continuum applications rather than a single domain-specific prototype. At the Edge, applications interact with physical devices and perform low-latency sensing or safety actions; at the Fog, they execute near-source coordination, mediation, and stream-processing logic; at the Cloud, they consolidate global knowledge through analytics, optimization, and visualization. This partitioning enables researchers to deploy, customize, and compare alternative control and monitoring strategies over the same programmable infrastructure substrate.
  The approach is validated through two representative use cases: Renewable Energy Community management, where distributed Digital Twin coordination and time-window-based energy control are requested, and AirWatch, a monitoring pipeline focused on anomaly detection, low-latency alerting, and cloud-side aggregation. Both workloads are evaluated through a systematic campaign of 40 runs comparing virtualized and physical edge deployments over a geographically distributed infrastructure.

</details>


### [131] [Queue-Theoretic Admission Control for Multi-Tenant GPU Clusters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28223)
*Sohan Kunkerkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 将GPU集群准入形式化为多类别多资源排队网络，证明待处理队列可分解为可报价工作负载（等待时间有界）和不可行工作负载（无有限界），建立O(1/(1-ρ))的等待时间缩放规律，并证明最优准入排序是NP难的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GPU集群操作员无法预测待处理工作负载的等待时间，现有系统使用贪心启发式方法，缺乏形式化的等待时间保证。

Method: 将GPU集群准入形式化为多类别多资源排队网络，证明结构分解定理；对可报价工作负载将每个集群队列建模为M/G/k系统，其中有效服务器数k由向量装箱归约确定；在随机支配假设下证明等待时间缩放规律；通过向量装箱问题归约证明最优准入排序的NP难性。

Result: 在Kueue上验证了方法的有效性：向量k_eff正确识别瓶颈资源维度，Little定律精确成立，Erlang-C近似一致地保守高估观察到的等待时间。

Conclusion: 所提出的理论框架为GPU集群准入提供了形式化的等待时间保证，能够有效区分可报价和不可行工作负载，并为实际系统提供保守的等待时间估计。

Abstract: GPU cluster operators cannot predict how long pending workloads will wait for admission. Existing systems use greedy heuristics with no formal wait time guarantees. We formalize GPU cluster admission as a multi-class, multi-resource queueing network and prove a structural decomposition: the pending queue partitions into quotable workloads (bounded wait time under stability) and unfeasible workloads (no finite bound without reconfiguration). For quotable workloads, we model each cluster queue as an M/G/k system where the effective server count k is determined by a vector packing reduction; under an explicit stochastic domination assumption, we establish O(1/(1-rho)) wait time scaling. We prove that optimal admission ordering is NP-hard under multi-dimensional resource demands via reduction from vector bin packing. We validate on Kueue, the standard Kubernetes workload queuing system, using CPU, memory, and GPU (via Dynamic Resource Allocation) resources. The vector k_eff correctly identifies bottleneck resource dimensions, Little's Law holds exactly, and the Erlang-C approximation consistently overestimates observed wait times in the conservative direction.

</details>


### [132] [FAIR-Compute: A Roadmap for Fair and Efficient Allocation of Federated Digital Research Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28290)
*Konstantinos,E. Zachariadis,Ahmed Sayed,Dimitris Fotakis,Angeliki Mathioudaki,Wan Shuen Siaw*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了联邦数字研究基础设施中的计算资源分配问题，发现现有调度存在测量偏差、简单启发式算法表现良好但联邦系统可能出现类似Braess悖论的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高性能计算需求的增长，稀缺计算资源的分配方式（而非仅仅是总量）已成为影响英国研究生产力的关键因素。一旦共享系统需要决定“谁在何时、基于何种证据运行”，其调度器设置就不再是纯粹的技术问题，而成为政策问题。

Method: 研究结合了三个证据线：1）对英国及国际分配实践（包括EuroHPC、WLCG、ACCESS、JASMIN和DiRAC）的景观审查，辅以利益相关者调查；2）在战略性和不确定用户报告下的分配机制设计模型；3）基于公开Fresco/Anvil工作负载轨迹和受控合成压力测试的仿真研究。

Result: 三项证据线得出三个共性结果：首先，分配记录测量的是占用率（预留资源）而非利用率（完成的有效工作），系统目前无法回答资源是否创造生产价值这一关键问题；其次，简单透明的调度启发式算法经调优后性能接近离线全信息基准，短期内应调优和监测现有调度器而非替换；第三，联邦化确有价值，但若不加管理则表现为非协调路由，可能在降低平均延迟的同时将负载和潜在危害集中于接收系统，这与Braess悖论相似。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了联邦数字研究基础设施分配中的系统性测量缺陷、现有调度器的优化潜力以及联邦化管理的风险，为未来资源分配政策制定提供了理论依据和实践指导，强调了需要从技术视角向政策视角转变。

Abstract: As demand for high-performance computing (HPC), high-throughput computing and data storage grows, the way scarce compute is allocated -- not just how much exists -- has become a decisive factor in the productivity of UK research. FAIR-Compute studies allocation in a federated Digital Research Infrastructure (DRI) as a problem at the intersection of algorithmic game theory, transport economics and HPC scheduling. Our central observation is simple: once a shared system must decide who runs, when and under what evidence, its scheduler settings cease to be a purely technical matter and become policy.
  The project combined three strands of evidence: a landscape review of UK and international allocation practice (including EuroHPC, WLCG, ACCESS, JASMIN and DiRAC) supported by a stakeholder survey; a mechanism-design model of allocation under strategic and uncertain user reports; and a simulation study built on the public Fresco/Anvil workload trace and controlled synthetic stress tests. Three results recur across all three strands. First, allocation records measure occupancy (resources reserved) rather than utilisation (useful work done), so the system cannot currently answer the question UKRI and DSIT most want answered -- whether resources deliver productive value. Second, simple, transparent scheduling heuristics perform close to an offline full-information benchmark once tuned, so the near-term opportunity is to tune and instrument existing schedulers rather than replace them. Third, federation is genuinely valuable but behaves like uncoordinated routing when left unmanaged: it can minimise average delay while quietly concentrating load -- and potentially harm -- on the receiving system. This mirrors a classic effect from road-traffic economics (Braess's paradox), where letting everyone independently pick the fastest route can leave the whole network worse off.

</details>


### [133] [A Taxonomy of Performance Metrics for the Distributed Computing Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28407)
*Praveen Kumar Donta,Boris Sedlak,Alfreds Lapkovskis,Alaa Saleh,Ying Li,Victor Casamayor Pujol,Ilir Murturi,Manuel Otero Barbasan,Schahram Dustdar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对分布式计算连续系统（DCCS）提出了一个结构化的性能指标分类体系，涵盖计算层、网络层和应用/用户层，并引入可持续性、可观测性、适应性等新兴维度，为跨层性能评估提供了统一框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能和大规模数据驱动应用的增长，应用任务在数据中心和边缘环境间日益分布式化，催生了新型计算连续体系结构和算法。然而现有评估实践往往只关注计算、网络、能效或应用质量等孤立维度，无法提供跨层DCCS行为的完整视图，因此亟需透明且一致的性能评估方法。

Method: 提出DCCS性能指标的结构化分类体系，将指标划分为计算层、网络层和应用/用户层类别，同时突出可持续性、可观测性、适应性、数据局部性、迁移感知和连续体碎片化等新兴维度。为异构和动态连续环境提供了数学表述，并从采集范围、采集阶段和测量方法三个维度总结指标获取要求。

Result: 建立了涵盖多维度和多层次的DCCS性能指标分类体系，为指标能否从单节点、多节点或全系统采集，以及更适合运营监测还是实验评估提供了清晰指导，填补了跨层性能评估的空白。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为理解和改进DCCS提供了系统性工具，有助于研究人员和实践者选择合适指标进行全面评估，促进计算连续系统的透明、一致性能评价。

Abstract: Performance evaluation is essential for understanding, comparing, and improving computing systems, including Distributed Computing Continuum Systems (DCCS). In recent years, computational requirements have changed substantially with the growth of artificial intelligence and large-scale data-driven applications. These application tasks are increasingly distributed between resource-intensive data centers and resource-constrained edge environments. In this context, novel computing continuum architectures and algorithms are emerging, creating a need for transparent and consistent performance evaluation. However, existing evaluation practices often focus on isolated dimensions, such as computation, networking, energy efficiency, or application-level quality, and therefore provide only a partial view of cross-layer DCCS behavior. This paper presents a structured taxonomy of performance metrics for DCCS. The taxonomy organizes metrics into computing-level, network-level, and application/user-level categories, while also highlighting emerging dimensions such as sustainability, observability, adaptability, data locality, migration awareness, and continuum fragmentation. Further, we provide mathematical formulations and discuss their relevance to heterogeneous and dynamic continuum environments. We also summarize metric acquisition requirements in terms of acquisition scope, acquisition phase, and measurement method. These requirements help clarify whether a metric can be collected from a single node, multiple nodes, or the full system, and whether it is more suitable for operational monitoring or experimental evaluation.

</details>


### [134] [Safe Quotes for Retroactive Liquidity Pools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28522)
*Peter Bro Miltersen*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文证明了在一般情况下为追溯式常数乘积流动性池计算安全报价是NP难问题，不存在具有固定近似比的多项式时间算法，但提出了两个实际可行的近似算法来绕过这一障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨链或分片环境下的自动化做市商（AMM）无法原子化执行交易序列。Aanes等人引入了锁交易（lock-swaps）和追溯式常数乘积流动性池来提供价格保证，但其算法在流动性提供与回收请求并存时会失效，且该情况的计算复杂性当时尚未解决。本文旨在解决这一开放问题并给出实用的解决方案。

Method: 首先，通过将经典的NP完全问题规约到该问题，证明了在一般情况下计算安全报价的近似难度：除非P=NP，不存在具有固定乘法近似比的多项式时间算法。其次，提出了两个简单实用的算法，其近似比虽然依赖于输入，但在实际场景中能够给出令人满意的报价。

Result: 证明了计算一般情况下的安全报价是NP难的，且不存在具有固定近似比的多项式时间近似算法。同时，设计了两个实用的近似算法，它们能够在实践中有效工作，输入依赖的近似比在实际场景中表现良好。

Conclusion: 虽然理论结果表明在一般情形下精确或固定近似比的安全报价计算不可行，但所提出的实用算法能够有效绕过这一障碍，使得锁交易功能在实际部署中仍然可行，为跨链AMM提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Automated market makers exchange assets through liquidity pools whose quoted prices depend on their reserves, with constant product pools being the most common. When such pools reside on different blockchains or shards, a sequence of swaps cannot in general be executed atomically. Aanes et al. introduced lock-swaps and retroactive constant product liquidity pools to provide price guarantees for such a setting. A retroactive pool implicitly maintains a virtual pool for each possible execute/cancel resolution of its active locks. In the presence of active locks, serving a new swap request requires computing a safe quote; a quote with an output that does not exceed the minimum possible output, taken over all virtual pools. The quote being safe is a hard constraint ensuring the integrity of the pool. A soft constraint is to make the quote as close to the minimum possible output as possible. Aanes et al. gave a simple and efficient algorithm for computing the exact minimum when unresolved provides and reclaims of liquidity do not coexist, showed by an explicit example that the algorithm fails in general, and left the computational complexity of the general case open. In this paper, we show that unless P is equal to NP, there is no polynomial time algorithm that computes in the general case a safe quote with any fixed multiplicative approximation ratio (e.g., 50%) relative to the exact minimum. This seems like a severe obstacle for deployment of the lock-swap functionality. However, we also present two simple and practical algorithms for computing safe quotes that have input-dependent approximation ratios that are likely to be satisfactory in practice, thus circumventing that obstacle.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Multi-Head Attention Residuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27230)
*Cheng Luo,Zefan Cai,Junjie Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多头注意力残差（MHAR），通过将路由查询重塑为H个子空间头，每个头独立学习深度历史上的softmax分布，在100M-1B规模上显著改善验证损失，并在8B模型上提升GSM8K和GPQA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer通过单一加性残差流传播信息，每个子层仅读取最新状态。注意力残差虽允许子层读取深度历史，但使用单一查询在宽度上共享，强制所有特征子空间通过同一分布读取历史。这种强制妥协的代价随子空间对读取层选择的不一致程度增长，而不一致性随模型宽度增加。

Method: 引入多头注意力残差（MHAR）：将路由查询重塑为H个子空间头，每个头在深度历史上拥有独立的softmax分布。读取变为块对角形式，重塑零参数增加且计算开销可忽略，H=1时精确恢复注意力残差。采用融合Triton路由内核提升训练吞吐量，并使用保持恒等性的delta注意力残差转换支持大规模训练。

Result: 在100M、350M、1B规模上验证损失分别降低0.061、0.149、0.140。在四种方法中各规模均获最佳结果，增益从100M到更大规模递增。H=4或H=8为最优（平坦），H=16过度分割会回退部分增益。8B模型在GSM8K和GPQA上分别提升+3.2和+3.1。Triton内核使训练吞吐量从0.2-0.5x提升至0.55-0.88x。

Conclusion: 头数H是真实的设计维度而非自由参数，验证损失对H呈U型曲线。学习的子空间不一致性是驱动因素的根本原因。MHAR在保持近基准峰值内存的同时实现显著性能提升，为大规模语言模型提供有效的架构改进。

Abstract: Transformers propagate information across depth through a single additive residual stream: every sublayer reads only the most recent state. Attention residuals relax this by letting each sublayer attend, through a learned softmax. However, that read uses a single query shared across the entire width, so every feature subspace must read the depth history through one distribution. The cost of this forced compromise grows with how much the subspaces disagree about which layers to read, and disagreement grows with model width. We introduce Multi-Head Attention Residuals (MHAR): the routing query is reshaped into H per-subspace heads, each with its own softmax over the depth history. The read becomes block-diagonal, the reshape adds zero parameters and negligible compute, and H = 1 recovers attention residuals exactly. Trained from scratch on a deduplicated Nemotron-based anneal corpus that is quality-filtered and STEM- and code-heavy, MHAR improves validation loss over a standard Transformer at 100M, 350M, and 1B (-0.061, -0.149, and -0.140). It achieves the best result among four methods in every setting, with the gain increasing from 100M to the larger scales. The head count is a real design axis rather than a free knob: validation loss is U-shaped with respect to H, with a flat optimum at H = 4 or H = 8 across scales. We adopt H = 8 for large-scale models; over-splitting beyond this point (H = 16) consistently gives back part of the gain. A direct probe of the trained queries confirms that learned subspace disagreement is the underlying driver. Fused Triton routing kernels increase attention-residual training throughput from 0.2-0.5x to 0.55-0.88x of the baseline while maintaining near-baseline peak memory. An identity-preserving conversion using delta attention residuals supports 8B mid-training, yielding improvements of +3.2 on GSM8K and +3.1 on GPQA.

</details>


### [136] [KernelGenBench: A Multi-Source and Multi-Chip Benchmark for LLM-based Kernel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27231)
*Peiyu Zang,Jian Tao,Jialing Zhang,Yichen Yuan,Wentao Zhang,Guang Liu,Yonghua Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了KernelGenBench，首个系统评估LLM/Agent生成Triton内核的统一基准，包含多源(210算子)和多芯片(6平台110算子)两个子基准，大规模评估发现Agent方法优于纯LLM、跨平台性能下降严重、生成成本高昂。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs显著增加了对高效加速器内核的需求，但内核开发仍是高度专业化且劳动密集型任务。尽管LLM和Agent框架为自动内核生成提供了可能，但目前缺乏跨多样算子源和异构硬件平台全面评估LLM生成内核的基准。

Method: 提出KernelGenBench统一基准，包含两个互补子基准：(1) KernelGenBench-MS评估来自PyTorch之外三种源的210个算子；(2) KernelGenBench-MC使用110算子子集测量六个异构硬件平台的性能可移植性。进行大规模评估，消耗超过150亿tokens。

Result: (1)基于Agent的方法持续优于纯LLM采样，cuBLAS算子对所有方法最具挑战性；(2)生成性能在硬件平台间差异显著，专门化Agent也存在严重跨平台退化（如AutoKernel在NVIDIA达87%，在平台E降至25%）；(3)自主内核生成成本高昂，专门Agent方法平均每个成功算子消耗511万tokens，比简单LLM采样高数个数量级。

Conclusion: 当前LLM/Agent内核生成技术虽展现潜力但仍面临重大挑战：跨平台性能移植性差、生成成本极高、特定算子类型（如cuBLAS）难度大，需进一步研究提升效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have significantly increased the demand for efficient accelerator kernels, but kernel development remains a highly specialized and labor-intensive task. The recent rise of LLMs and agentic frameworks offers a promising pathway toward automatic kernel generation. However, despite rapid progress, there is still no comprehensive benchmark to rigorously evaluate LLM-generated kernels across diverse operator sources or heterogeneous hardware platforms. We present KernelGenBench, a unified benchmark for systematically evaluating LLM- and agent-generated Triton kernels across diverse operator sources and heterogeneous hardware platforms. It comprises two complementary sub-benchmarks: KernelGenBench-MS (Multi-Source), evaluating 210 operators from three sources beyond standard PyTorch-centric tasks, and KernelGenBench-MC (Multi-Chip), measuring performance portability across six heterogeneous hardware platforms using a 110-operator subset. Our large-scale evaluation, consuming over 15 billion tokens, shows: (1) agent-based methods consistently outperform pure LLM sampling methods, while cuBLAS operators are the most challenging across all methods; (2) generation performance varies significantly across hardware platforms, with even recent kernel-specialized agents experiencing severe cross-platform degradation (e.g., AutoKernel drops from 87% on NVIDIA to 25% on Platform E); (3) autonomous kernel generation remains highly cost-intensive, with specialized agent methods averaging 5.11 million tokens per successful operator (AKO4all reaches 5.19 million), orders of magnitude higher than simple LLM sampling approaches.

</details>


### [137] [RadHarmony: Radiological Data Handling in the Era of Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27235)
*Frank Li,Bardia Khosravi,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Theo Dapamede,YoungSeok Jeon,Janice Newsome,Hari Trivedi,Judy Gichoya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了RadHarmony，一个开源Python库，用于统一加载、协调和增强放射学数据集，支持24个公共数据集的元数据标准化和多种标注类型，并提供了AI代理辅助新数据集集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在放射学图像上训练深度学习模型需要整合来自不同来源、文件格式、目录结构、标签模式和注释类型的异构数据集，这给研究带来了巨大挑战。

Method: 开发RadHarmony开源Python库，将24个公共数据集的元数据标准化为单一表格格式，封装MONAI的map-style数据集，支持分类标签、分割掩码、边界框和放射学报告文本，并引入AI代理技能指导新数据集集成工作流程。

Result: 成功实现了多源异构放射学数据集的统一API接口，通过预训练RadHarmony-ViT视觉Transformer基线模型验证了库的实用性，该模型结合了三个异构胸部X光数据集且无需数据集特定代码。

Conclusion: RadHarmony有效降低了放射学数据集整合的技术门槛，为深度学习模型训练提供了统一、高效的数据处理解决方案，促进了放射学AI研究的可重复性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Training deep learning models on radiological images requires integrating heterogeneous datasets across different sources, file formats, directory layouts, label schemas, and annotation types. We present RadHarmony, an open-source Python library that provides a unified API for loading, harmonizing, and augmenting radiological datasets, with a primary focus on chest radiographs and early support for computed tomography (CT) and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI). RadHarmony standardizes metadata from 24 public datasets into a single tabular format, wraps MONAI's map-style datasets for deep-learning-ready sample delivery with optional on-disk caching, and supports classification labels, segmentation masks, bounding boxes, and radiology report text through a single interface, with an interactive visualization tool for dataset exploration and verification. To lower the barrier for integrating new datasets, RadHarmony introduces an AI-agent skill that guides the full integration workflow from raw data inspection through code generation and testing. We demonstrate the library's utility by pretraining RadHarmony-ViT, a reference vision transformer baseline that combines three heterogeneous chest radiograph datasets with no dataset-specific code. The code and pretrained model weights are available at https://github.com/f10409/RadHarmony.

</details>


### [138] [Asymmetric Collapse in Model Merging: When Refusal Over- writes Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27240)
*Aarnav Choudhary,Matheus Fonseca Rocha,Jiwon Seo,Vasu Sharma,Maheep Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.AI
