Conclusion: 本文证明了在Lean中构建的双层验证框架既能确保约束问题重写的语义正确性，又能高效验证求解器输出，实现了实用的端到端可信工作流，为约束规划的形式化验证提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Constraint programming is a core technology for solving complex combinatorial problems in scheduling, planning, configuration, and verification. Trusting its results therefore demands guarantees at two levels: that reformulations applied beforehand are semantics-preserving, and that solvers produce correct answers. In this work, we introduce a framework that addresses both verification levels in the Lean theorem prover: it can be used to prove formulation-level properties, such as equivalence, equisatisfiability, and the correctness of symmetry-breaking constraints, parametrically for entire problem families; and to check solver-produced certificates for individual instances via translation backends to external formats such as MiniZinc, SMT-LIB, and OPB. Combining both levels yields an end-to-end workflow that establishes the satisfiability or unsatisfiability of a constraint problem without trusting the external solver. Experimental results show that our framework's verified symmetry breaking also pays off in practice: a single parametric proof per problem family, reused across all instance sizes, reduces solver search effort by a factor of up to 2x10^7, while the entire in-Lean certification stays affordable, taking at most a few minutes for our largest instances.

</details>


### [244] [A report-grounded vision-language foundation model for colonoscopy from 280000 routine reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28466)
*Jia Yu,Yan Zhu,Yili He,Zilong Wang,Xinyang Jiang,Peiyao Fu,Ruijie Yang,Tianyi Chen,Siyuan Li,Zhihua Wang,Fei Wu,Quanlin Li,Xian Yang,Pinghong Zhou,Shuo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EndoCLIP，一种基于常规结肠镜检查记录训练的视觉-语言基础模型，通过恢复病灶与图像帧的对应关系，在多个临床任务中表现优异，接近专家水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管常规结肠镜检查报告包含丰富的专家描述（如病变外观、大小和位置），但这些报告通常总结整个手术过程，而非为单帧图像提供标注，导致临床发现与对应图像之间联系薄弱。视觉-语言模型在结肠镜领域尚未得到充分利用。

Method: 开发了EndoCLIP模型，从280,476份常规结肠镜检查记录中逐步恢复出125,756对病灶级别的图像-文本对，并在此基础上训练视觉-语言基础模型。

Result: 在病灶级别图文检索、结构化报告生成以及六个多中心临床分类任务中，EndoCLIP在零样本和线性探测设置下均优于通用及生物医学视觉-语言编码器。在良恶性分类任务中，其线性探测性能接近12名内镜专家的盲研水平。

Conclusion: 恢复病灶与图像帧的对应关系可将常规文档转化为可扩展的监督信号，使临床目标能够通过语言指定，而无需为每个任务单独标注。

Abstract: Vision-language models remain underused in colonoscopy despite the rich expert descriptions recorded in routine reports. These reports document lesion appearance, size and location but summarise entire procedures rather than caption individual frames, leaving clinical findings only weakly linked to the corresponding images. Here we develop EndoCLIP, a colonoscopy vision-language foundation model trained on 125,756 lesion-level image-text pairs progressively recovered from 280,476 routine colonoscopy records. Across lesion-level image-text retrieval, structured report generation and six multi-centre clinical classification tasks, EndoCLIP outperforms general-purpose and biomedical vision-language encoders in both zero-shot and linear-probe settings. On benign-versus-malignant classification, its linear probe approaches the performance of expert readers in a blinded study involving 12 endoscopists. These results suggest that recovering finding-to-frame correspondence can transform routine documentation into scalable supervision, enabling clinical targets to be specified in language rather than separately annotated for each task.

</details>


### [245] [Towards Autonomous Aircraft Surveillance from Nanosatellites through On-Board Inference and Generative Data Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28470)
*Antonio Delgado-Rosa,David Muñoz-Valero,Enrique Adrian Villarrubia-Martin,Juan Moreno-Garcia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合星载推理与生成式数据增强的工作流，在6U立方星上部署量化检测器，同时利用扩散模型生成合成样本以解决数据类别不平衡问题，显著提升了罕见飞机类别的检测性能并满足实时处理需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对纳米卫星对地观测中存在的两个核心瓶颈：有限的下行链路带宽与海量原始数据传输之间的矛盾，以及公开飞机检测数据集稀缺且类别严重不平衡，导致标准检测器难以学习罕见类别的鲁棒特征。

Method: 在配备低功耗边缘张量加速器的6U立方星上执行量化目标检测推理；采用低秩适应微调的扩散模型生成合成少数类图像；通过中间检测器对合成图像进行自动伪标注，并与传统增强样本合并构建平衡数据集。

Result: 平衡数据集使全局平均精度从77.9%提升至82.2%，少数类F1分数从0.683提升至0.811；量化检测器可装入片上内存并实现25-30帧/秒的星载处理速度。

Conclusion: 该方法突破了传统“弯管”架构中卫星仅作为被动数据收集者的局限，验证了将计算前置至轨道进行实时自主空中监视的可行性，为纳米卫星决策支持系统提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Airborne surveillance from low Earth orbit is hindered by two interconnected bottlenecks: nanosatellites have a limited downlink budget, yet the conventional approach still transmits terabytes of raw imagery to the ground for processing, and open satellite datasets for aircraft are scarce and severely class-imbalanced. These limitations either delay timely decision-making or prevent standard detectors from learning robust representations of rare aircraft classes. In this paper, a workflow that combines on-board inference with generative data augmentation is proposed to address both limitations jointly. Inference is executed on a 6U CubeSat equipped with a low-power edge tensor accelerator, while a diffusion model fine-tuned through low-rank adaptation generates synthetic minority-class imagery. This synthetic output is automatically annotated, pseudo-labelled, by an intermediate detector and merged with classically augmented samples. The results show that the balanced dataset increases global mean average precision from 77.9% to 82.2%, with the minority class rising from F1=0.683 to F1=0.811, and that the quantised detector fits the on-chip memory and projects 25-30 frames per second on orbit. This approach contrasts with the conventional bent-pipe architecture, in which the satellite acts as a passive data collector. Therefore, the computational tests support the proposed workflow as a decision-support tool for real-time, autonomous airborne surveillance from nanosatellites.

</details>


### [246] [A Fuzzy Rule-based Neuro-Symbolic Approach for Pipe Severity Prediction in Sewer Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28481)
*Ngoc Thai Le,Thanh Ma,Umberto Straccia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出模糊规则神经符号框架用于下水管道严重性评估，相比图像分类方法准确率提升17.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自动管道严重性评估依赖直接图像分类形成"黑箱"，视觉缺陷与最终严重性评分之间的联系不明确，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 模块化框架：感知模块用Swin Transformer预测14个多标签CODE等级；推理模块用决策树生成19条IF-THEN规则，通过模糊逻辑（t-norm/s-norm）进行可解释推理。

Result: 在3244张图像数据集上，准确率、平衡准确率、宏F1和MCC分别提升17.9%、12.2%、23.0%和17.3%，同时实现可追溯的推理过程。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持竞争力的类别平衡性能的同时，实现了从预测CODE等级到规则支持再到严重性证据的可追溯推理，成功结合了神经感知与符号推理。

Abstract: Standard automated sewer pipe severity assessment relies on direct image classification, creating a "black box" where the link between visual defects and final severity scores remains implicit. This study introduces a modular, fuzzy rule-based neuro-symbolic framework that bridges this gap by decoupling neural perception from symbolic reasoning. The perception module utilizes a Swin Transformer to predict 14 multilabel inspection CODE degrees directly from images. For reasoning, a DT, specifically Weka's J48, algorithm is trained on ground-truth CODEs and severity labels, and its paths are converted into 19 fixed IF--THEN rules. Inference operates via fuzzy logic: t-norm activations from CODE conditions are weighted by rule confidence and combined with corresponding s-norms to produce interpretable class evidence. We assessed Product, Łukasiewicz, and Hamacher operator pairs using a dataset of 3,244 images spanning five highly imbalanced severity classes. Ground-truth labels were robustly generated via consensus from five independent large language models analyzing original inspector notes. Our results show an improvement of accuracy, balanced accuracy, Macro F1 and MCC by 17.9%, 12.2%, 23.0%, and 17.3%, respectively, over image-only based classification.
  Overall, the framework combines competitive class-balanced performance with traceable reasoning from predicted CODE degrees to rule supports and severity evidence.

</details>


### [247] [SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28488)
*Yunhao Liang,Xianqi Cao,Pujun Zhang,Yuan Qu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Max Z. J. Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SCOPE框架，通过统一的端到端协调模型解决供应链中各决策模块孤立优化的问题，在生鲜零售补货场景中显著优于传统分阶段优化方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代供应链中的选品、供应商分配、补货频率和配送路线等关键决策通常由不同部门独立优化，但这些决策在操作上紧密耦合：选品影响后续阶段的需求和负载，供应商分配和补货频率改变配送请求，而路线可行性和成本又反过来影响前期决策的价值。这种孤立优化导致缺货、库存积压和运输浪费等问题。

Method: 提出SCOPE（Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination）复合策略模型：将供应链实体表示为token，通过共享的操作表示进行上下文建模，每种token类型映射到相应的决策接口；每个决策基于前期决策形成的部分计划，完整计划通过共享的系统级效用函数评估；实现跨部门决策的端到端协调。

Result: 在叮咚买菜和京东两个大规模供应链的真实运营数据上进行验证，涵盖不同补货层级；SCOPE在两种场景下均一致优于分阶段单独优化的方法以及供应链运营中常用的实践基准方法。

Conclusion: 学习和协调跨部门操作耦合能够实现更有效的端到端供应链决策，证明了统一运营规划优于孤立决策模块的价值。

Abstract: Can supply-chain AI move beyond isolated decision modules toward unified operational planning? A complete replenishment plan specifies which products each location carries, which upstream facility supplies it, how often it is replenished, and how deliveries are routed. These decisions are operationally coupled: the selected assortment changes the demand and load passed to later stages; source assignment and replenishment frequency reshape the delivery requests; and route feasibility and cost, in turn, determine the system value of the earlier choices. Yet in modern supply chains, these decisions are often handled by separate departments and optimized through separate systems, which can lead to stockouts, inventory exposure, and avoidable transportation. We propose SCOPE: Supply-Chain Operations through Coupled Policies for End-to-End Coordination, a composite policy model that represents supply-chain entities as tokens, contextualizes them through a shared operational representation, and maps each token type to the corresponding decision interface. Each decision builds on the partial plan formed by earlier decisions while the completed plan is evaluated using a shared system-level utility. We instantiate this framework in urban fresh-retail replenishment, where service frequency, assortment, capacity pressure, and road-network routing interact strongly, and evaluate it on real operational data from Dingdong and JD.com, two large-scale supply chains operating at different replenishment echelons. Across both settings, SCOPE consistently outperforms methods that optimize each decision stage separately, as well as practice-oriented baselines commonly used in supply-chain operations. These results show that learning and coordinating cross-department operational couplings lead to more effective end-to-end supply-chain decisions.

</details>


### [248] [InfoOps Bench: A live information operations safety benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28503)
*Dorian Quelle,Lisa-Maria Neudert,Jonathan Bright,John Gallacher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了一个动态更新的AI基准测试，用于评估前沿语言模型被国家支持的信息操作利用的风险，测试了17个模型，发现完整率差异巨大（8.8%-94.5%），并发现中国模型在涉及中国的批评性内容上存在显著的合规下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型的广泛部署，它们可能被恶意行为者利用来生成和传播虚假信息，特别是国家支持的信息操作。为了确保AI系统的安全性和完整性，需要建立一个实时更新的基准测试来持续评估模型抵抗被用于信息操作的能力，而不是静态的一次性评估。

Method: 研究团队构建了一个动态基准测试InfoOpsBench，从实时监控管线中提取超过2,100个俄罗斯、中国和伊朗国家支持的信息操作案例。测试了来自8个提供商的17个模型，采用四种不同的提示框架，测试模型是否会拒绝生成信息操作内容。完整性评分定义为拒绝请求的百分比。同时发布了配套网站每周追踪最突出的信息操作声明。

Result: 测试显示大多数模型可以被用于信息操作，完整率从8.8%到94.5%不等，差异达85.7个百分点，且与模型大小无关。不同模型产生的内容特征各异：有的虚构细节产生比源材料更有害的内容，有的在合规时淡化声明，事实核查率从2.9%到72.9%。中国开发的模型（除Z.ai的GLM 5.2外）在对中国的批评性声明上合规率大幅下降48-70个百分点。安全性与可用性之间存在权衡困境。

Conclusion: 研究表明当前前沿语言模型普遍存在被用于国家信息操作的风险，完整率差异巨大且模型行为特征各异。动态基准测试能够持续监测这一风险。研究揭示了模型安全性与可用性之间的基本张力，以及特定国家开发的模型在处理敏感地缘政治内容时表现出的偏差，为AI安全治理提供了重要见解。

Abstract: In this paper we present an active, constantly updated AI benchmark which measures the integrity of frontier language models against being co-opted for state-backed information operations. We draw on over 2,100 information operations from a live monitoring pipeline which tracks Russian, Chinese and Iranian state-backed information assets. Alongside this paper, we release a companion website that tracks the most prominent claims spread by state-backed media outlets, updated weekly, available from: pattrn.ai/research/infoopsbench. The dynamic nature of the benchmark makes it resistant to saturation.
  In the benchmark, we test 17 models from 8 providers across four prompt framings. We find that most models can be co-opted for information operations. Integrity scores, defined as the percentage of refused requests, range from 8.8% to 94.5%, an 85.7-percentage-point spread not explained by model size. Model choice also changes the character of the resulting operation. Some models fabricate details and produce output more harmful than the source material, others defuse claims even while complying, and fact-checking rates vary from 2.9% to 72.9%.
  Integrity against information operations is at least partly related to refusal to produce content even for benign claims, illustrating the challenge of balancing model usability with safety. With one exception (Z.ai's GLM 5.2), the Chinese-developed models sharply cut compliance on factually grounded but China-critical claims, dropping 48-70 percentage points relative to matched benign claims.

</details>


### [249] [Selective Credibility-Limited Belief Update](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28523)
*Theofanis Aravanis,Costas D. Koutras*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了选择性可信限制信念更新框架，通过在执行可信限制转换前将认知输入转换为较弱的代理，解决了现有方法无法处理复合认知输入部分实现的问题，并证明了该框架统一且严格扩展了现有的信念更新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可信限制信念更新方法将认知输入视为不可分割的整体，无法表示只有复合认知输入的一部分能够实现的情况。例如，当代理接收到一个由多个命题组成的复合输入时，可能只有其中部分命题在当前世界状态下是可信的，而现有方法无法捕捉这种选择性接受的行为。

Method: 引入选择性可信限制信念更新机制，针对每个源世界，将原始认知输入转换为较弱的代理（proxy），然后再执行可信限制转换。具体方法包括：定义转换函数将认知输入映射为其可信后果；提出语义刻画和公理化刻画；识别两个良好行为的子类——一致性保持更新算子（要求每当原始输入一致时，转换后的输入必须从其源世界可信）和最大一致性保持更新算子（额外要求所选代理在原始输入的可信后果中具有最大信息量）。

Result: 提供了选择性可信限制信念更新算子的语义和公理化刻画；证明可信限制信念更新是该框架的特例；证明当移除可信限制且转换函数取恒等映射时，Katsuno-Mendelzon信念更新作为特例出现；证明了该框架的严格更强表达能力。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架提供了信念更新的统一且严格更具表达力的刻画，既涵盖已有方法又支持依赖源世界的选择性接受，为处理认知输入的部分实现问题提供了形式化工具，丰富了信念更新的理论基础。

Abstract: Belief update concerns changes in an agent's beliefs induced by changes in the underlying world. Standard Katsuno-Mendelzon update assumes that an epistemic input can be incorporated from every initially possible world, whereas credibility-limited belief update restricts, for each source world, the successor worlds regarded as credible or reachable. Nevertheless, existing credibility-limited approaches treat the epistemic input as an indivisible whole, and therefore cannot represent cases in which only part of a compound epistemic input can be realized. We introduce selective credibility-limited belief update, in which the epistemic input is transformed, relative to each source world, into a weaker proxy before the credibility-limited transition is performed. We provide semantic and axiomatic characterizations of the resulting class of update operators. We then identify two well-behaved sub-classes; namely, consistency-preserving update operators, which require every transformed epistemic input to be credible from its source world whenever the original epistemic input is consistent, and maximal consistency-preserving update operators, which additionally require the selected proxy to be maximally informative among the credible consequences of the original epistemic input. Finally, we establish the generality of the proposed framework by showing that credibility-limited belief update is recovered as a special case, while Katsuno--Mendelzon belief update emerges when credibility restrictions are removed and the transformation functions are taken to be identities. These results demonstrate that the framework provides a unified and strictly more expressive account of belief update, encompassing established approaches while supporting source-dependent selective acceptance.

</details>


### [250] [MANTA: Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation for Self-Evolving Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28527)
*Mao-xun Huang,Jerry Wang,Yi-Cheng Lai,Zhengxin Zhang,Claire Cardie,Hen-Hsen Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MANTA框架实现了多智能体系统通信拓扑在推理时的自适应演化，在五个基准测试中取得74.0的平均最高分，超越最强基线5.8个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于大语言模型的多智能体系统将通信拓扑视为固定设计或离线优化目标，无法在推理过程中根据实际需求动态调整协作结构，限制了复杂问题求解的效果。

Method: 提出MANTA（Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation）框架，包含两个阶段：执行前根据历史结构经验初始化任务条件化的拓扑；部署期间监控协作轨迹，当当前组织结构不足时应用有界的结构更新，可修改智能体角色、通信链接、执行顺序、信息可见性和验证路径，同时保持任务接口和智能体预算不变。

Result: 在信息检索、工具使用、规划、工作流执行和数学推理等五个基准测试中，MANTA达到74.0的平均最高分，超越最强基线5.8个百分点，并在PlanCraft任务上取得最佳结果。

Conclusion: 推理时的自我改进能力可以扩展到协作架构本身，证明了动态演化多智能体通信拓扑的有效性和实用性。

Abstract: Large language model-based multi-agent systems improve complex problem solving through task decomposition, agent specialization, information exchange, and intermediate validation. However, existing systems typically treat communication topology as a fixed design choice or an offline optimization target. We introduce MANTA, a framework for Multi-Agent Network Topology Adaptation that enables communication structures to self-evolve at inference time. Before execution, MANTA initializes a task-conditioned topology from prior structural experience. During deployment, it monitors collaboration traces and applies bounded structural updates when the current organization becomes insufficient. These updates can modify agent roles, communication links, execution order, information visibility, and validation pathways while preserving the task interface and agent budget. We evaluate MANTA against representative single-agent and multi-agent baselines on five benchmarks spanning information seeking, tool use, planning, workflow execution, and mathematical reasoning. MANTA achieves the highest average score of 74.0, outperforming the strongest baseline by 5.8 percentage points and obtaining the best result on PlanCraft. These results show that inference-time self-improvement can extend to the architecture of collaboration itself.

</details>


### [251] [Rethinking Inference-Time Scaling in Local Computer-Use Agents: Failure Modes and Compute Tradeoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28573)
*Woongkyu Lee,Jungwook Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了推理时扩展（inference-time scaling）在本地资源受限计算机使用智能体（CUAs）中的表现，发现增加计算量往往收益递减，并会改变失败模式；建议未来需设计选择性计算分配与失败感知的控制机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 本地部署计算机使用智能体对隐私、成本和实用性至关重要，但在严格硬件约束下提升性能仍具挑战。虽然推理时扩展能提升前沿模型表现，但其对资源受限本地模型的效果尚不明确，因此需系统性研究其在本地CUAs中的作用。

Method: 在OSWorld基准上，对Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B、UI-TARS-1.5-7B和OpenCUA-7B三类本地模型，从上下文（contextual）、时间（temporal）、结构（structural）和并行（parallel）四个维度系统评估推理时扩展的效果。

Result: 推理时扩展在本地模型上往往收益递减且改变失败模式：上下文扩展提升轨迹稳定性和准确率但收益随token成本增加而饱和，失败模式从停滞转向假成功；时间扩展减少停滞却不显著提升成功率；结构分解引入额外开销；并行扩展部分缓解失败但计算成本高昂。

Conclusion: 高效的本地CUAs需要选择性计算分配、失败感知的控制机制，以及围绕本地模型能力与限制设计的智能体框架，不能简单套用前沿模型的扩展策略。

Abstract: Deploying autonomous computer-use agents (CUAs) locally is increasingly important for privacy, cost efficiency, and practical usability, yet improving their performance under strict hardware constraints remains challenging. While recent studies show that inference-time scaling can improve frontier computer-use agents through additional computation during execution, its effectiveness for resource-constrained local models remains poorly understood. We present a systematic empirical study of inference-time scaling in local CUAs across contextual, temporal, structural, and parallel dimensions. We evaluate Qwen3-VL-8B/30B-A3B, UI-TARS-1.5-7B, and OpenCUA-7B on the OSWorld benchmark. Our results show that additional computation often yields diminishing returns while changing failure modes. Contextual scaling provides historical grounding that improves trajectory stability and task accuracy, but its gains saturate as token cost increases and failures shift from repetitive or stalled trajectories toward premature false successes. Temporal scaling similarly reduces max-step stalls, yet does not substantially improve task success, indicating that longer horizons often extend erroneous trajectories rather than correct them. We further find that structural decomposition can introduce planning and formatting overhead in local two-stage agents, while parallel scaling partially mitigates these failures at a substantial computational cost. Overall, our findings suggest that efficient local CUAs require selective compute allocation, failure-aware control mechanisms, and agentic frameworks designed around the capabilities and limitations of local models.

</details>


### [252] [DualG-MRAG: Decoupling Macro-Reasoning and Micro-Matching for Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28580)
*Jiacheng Tao,Qingyun Sun,Haonan Yuan,Ziwei Zhang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出DualG-MRAG双层级框架，通过宏图和微图的解耦架构解决多模态RAG在复杂多跳推理中的图扩展噪声与关键证据丢失问题，提升证据召回和问答准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态RAG方法在复杂多跳推理任务上表现不佳：独立实例级匹配难以捕获跨模态和跨文档的显式关系；图增强方法面临两难困境——引入细粒度视觉特征导致图快速扩展和检索噪声，而粗粒度表示则导致关键局部证据被丢弃。

Method: 提出DualG-MRAG双层级框架：1)构建宏图用于全局拓扑路由，微图用于精确局部验证，解耦全局结构推理与细粒度证据匹配；2)设计GNN检索器，将检索建模为查询驱动的消息传递过程，实现跨异构证据源的动态相关性传播；3)引入动态规划解码机制，从GNN前向传播中提取显式推理路径，替代孤立文档块的输入方式。

Result: 实验结果表明，DualG-MRAG在证据召回率和复杂问答准确性上均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解耦宏图推理与微图匹配的双层级架构，DualG-MRAG有效平衡了多模态RAG中结构建模的粒度问题，在抑制检索噪声的同时保留了关键局部证据，为生成模型提供了连贯的结构化指导。

Abstract: While Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Generation (MM-RAG) has shown promising results, it still struggles with complex multi-hop reasoning tasks. Existing methods primarily focus on independent instance-level matching, which often fails to capture explicit relationships across modalities and documents. Although Graph-enhanced methods introduce structural modeling, they face a fundamental challenge in multimodal scenarios: incorporating fine-grained visual features leads to rapid graph expansion and retrieval noise, whereas coarse-grained representations cause the discarding of critical local evidence. To address this dilemma, we propose DualG-MRAG, a Dual-tier framework that introduces a decoupled architecture comprising Macro-reasoning and Micro-matching Graphs for Multimodal RAG. Specifically, to suppress retrieval noise by isolating global structural reasoning from fine-grained evidence matching, we construct a Macro Graph for global topological routing and a Micro Graph for precise local verification. Subsequently, to enable dynamic relevance propagation across heterogeneous evidence sources, we formulate retrieval as a query-driven message passing process via a GNN Retriever. Furthermore, to provide the generative model with coherent structural guidance, we introduce a dynamic programming decoding mechanism that extracts explicit reasoning paths directly from the GNN's forward pass, replacing the standard input of isolated document chunks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DualG-MRAG outperforms baselines in both evidence recall and complex QA accuracy.

</details>


### [253] [OSReward: Instituting Standardized Evaluation for Cross-Platform Computer-Use Reward Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28609)
*Qiushi Sun,Kanzhi Cheng,Yian Wang,Bowen Yang,Hang Yan,Liheng Chen,Fangzhi Xu,Zichen Ding,Nuo Chen,Jialin Cao,Xingdong Gong,Zehao Li,Kaiming Jin,Xinfeng Yuan,Zhoumianze Liu,Jingyang Gong,Zhangyue Yin,Jiahui Gao,Zhiyong Wu,Tianbao Xie,Jianbing Zhang,Ben Kao,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了OSReward基准来系统评估VLM作为CUA轨迹评判者的可靠性，发现现有模型存在系统性宽松偏差，并发布了OS-Shepherd系列开源奖励模型以低成本可靠地替代商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体（CUA）轨迹验证对评估、数据筛选和强化学习至关重要，但人工验证无法规模化，学界转而使用视觉语言模型（VLM）作为评判者。然而，这些VLM评判者的可靠性从未被系统检验，这是一个被忽视的基础问题。

Method: 构建OSReward基准，包含多平台、多智能体骨干的真实轨迹，通过多阶段人工标注获得高质量真值标签；在此基础上构建困难集OSReward-Hard和细粒度评分集OSReward-Multi。系统评估现有VLM评判者，分析其偏差特性。随后构建10万条推理标注数据集OS-Shepherd-100K，训练9B和35B参数的开源奖励模型。

Result: 大规模评估显示：即使最先进的VLM评判者也未达到理想水平，存在系统性宽松偏差——倾向于将失败轨迹误判为成功；足够可靠的模型成本过高，而低成本开源模型性能落后。基于此，训练的OS-Shepherd开源奖励模型能以比前沿商业模型低30-60%的成本提供稳定可靠的奖励信号，性能可媲美商业评判者。

Conclusion: 当前VLM评判者在CUA轨迹验证中可靠性不足，存在系统性偏差。通过构建高质量基准和开源奖励模型，本研究为社区提供了低成本、可靠的解决方案，推动了CUA评估的规模化发展。研究发布的代码、基准、数据集和模型检查点可供社区使用。

Abstract: Computer-using agents (CUAs) are advancing rapidly across the digital world. A CUA trajectory records the agent's actions, states, and reasoning. Verifying whether it fulfilled the task instruction is central to CUA evaluation, data curation, and reinforcement learning. Neither human-written verifiers nor human annotators can provide such verification at scale, so the field increasingly turns to vision-language models (VLMs) as judges of CUA trajectories. But a fundamental question has long gone unexamined: are these VLM judges reliable enough? To study it systematically, we introduce OSReward, a realistic, high-quality benchmark that evaluates VLM judges on CUA trajectories. The trajectories come from diverse agent backbones executing human-verified instructions across platforms, then rigorously labeled with ground-truth verdicts through multi-stage human annotation. Building on it, we derive OSReward-Hard, a challenge set concentrating genuinely hard cases, and OSReward-Multi for fine-grained efficiency and alignment scoring. The most comprehensive evaluation of VLM judges to date finds even state-of-the-art models fall short of an ideal judge, sharing a systematic leniency bias that mislabels failed runs as successes. The few reliable enough to trust are too expensive to run at scale, while affordable open models trail far behind. To close this gap, we construct and release OS-Shepherd-100K, an open corpus of reasoning-annotated trajectory judgments for the CUA community. On it, we train OS-Shepherd (9B and 35B), open reward models that supply low-cost, stable, and reliable reward signals, matching commercial judges at 30-60% lower cost than the frontier. Extensive analyses further inform the design of reliable CUA reward at scale. Our code, benchmark, dataset, and model checkpoints are available at https://os-copilot.github.io/OSReward-Home/.

</details>


### [254] [AISPA: User-Centric System Prompt Auditing for Large Language Model Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28617)
*Xiangning Lin,Shenzhe Zhu,Shu Yang,Zhenyu Zhang,Haoqian Zhang,Yipeng Zhao,Chengxuan Qian,Tianwei Wang,Ziheng Zhang,Zhenlong Yuan,Dingcheng Wang,Juncheng Wu,Yuan Si,Jiaxin Liu,Baolong Bi,Robert Mahari,Tobin South,Dazza Greenwood,Zexue He,Rishi Bommasani,Sophia Kazinnik,Andreas Haupt,Samuele Marro,Erik Brynjolfsson,Alex Pentland,Jiaxin Pei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AISPA框架用于审计商业AI产品的系统提示词，分析了88款产品的3249条指令，发现虽然用户保护指令广泛采用但覆盖深度不足，约40%产品仍存在损害用户利益的指令，揭示了系统提示词需要更高透明度和标准化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统提示词在商业AI产品中被广泛使用，用于控制基础模型的行为，但很少向公众或监管机构披露，造成了严重的信任和问责缺失。现有AI系统中系统提示词的不透明性阻碍了用户和监管者理解AI系统的实际行为机制，亟需一个系统性的审计框架来评估这些隐藏的指令如何影响用户利益。

Method: 提出AISPA（人工智能系统提示词保证）框架，该框架从用户中心视角出发，检查系统提示词的特定部分，并沿着八个对用户重要的维度进行评估。研究团队收集了88个商业AI产品的系统提示词，提取了3249条指令，将每条指令分类为保护性（保护用户）或有问题的（损害用户利益）两类，并进行定量分析。

Result: 发现四项核心结果：1）系统提示词设计在不同产品和开发者间差异显著，部分组织平均每个产品超过60条保护性指令，而其他组织平均少于5条；2）保护性指令被广泛采用但覆盖深度不足：98.9%的产品至少包含一条保护性指令，但只有24%覆盖了AISPA分类法的所有八个维度；3）系统提示词持续变长且更加保护用户，表明用户保护在商业提示词设计中日益受到重视；4）尽管有所进步，有问题的指令仍然普遍存在：约40%的产品包含至少一条损害用户利益的指令，且保护性和有问题的指令常在同一提示词中共存。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了商业AI产品中系统提示词的当前状态，强调了在系统提示词设计方面需要更高透明度、标准化和独立监督。保护性指令的广泛但浅层采用，以及有问题的指令的持续存在，表明现有实践仍不足以充分保护用户利益，需要建立更完善的审计和治理机制。

Abstract: System prompts are instructions configured by developers to govern the behaviors of foundation models in AI applications. They are used throughout commercial AI products, but are rarely disclosed to the public or regulators, creating a serious trust and accountability gap in the wide deployment of AI systems. In this paper, we introduce Artificial Intelligence System Prompt Assurance (AISPA), a user-centric framework for systematically auditing system prompts in AI systems. AISPA examines specific parts of a system prompt and evaluates them along eight dimensions that matter to users. We then use this framework to review 3,249 instructions from system prompts in 88 commercial AI products, classifying each instruction as either protective (of users) or problematic. Our audit surfaces four core findings. First, system prompt design varies substantially across products and developers, with some organizations averaging over 60 protective instructions per product while others average fewer than 5. Second, protective instructions are widely adopted but shallow in scope: 98.9% of products contain at least one, yet only 24% cover all eight dimensions of the AISPA taxonomy. Third, system prompts have grown steadily longer and more protective of users, suggesting that user protection is becoming a more visible concern in commercial prompt design. Fourth, despite this progress, problematic instructions remain pervasive: roughly 40% of products contain at least one instruction that works against user interests, and protective and problematic instructions frequently coexist within the same prompt. Our findings highlight the need for greater transparency, standardization, and independent oversight for system prompts in commercial AI products.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [255] [Auditing Emergent LLM-Agent Collaboration through Cooperation-Obligation Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27429)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Hanqing Yang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出iCORE框架，通过统一编码交互、义务和审计映射，解决LLM智能体系统的可审计性问题，显著提升任务质量和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-agent系统通过动态自组织和涌现协作解决复杂任务，但现有方法在审计过程中存在缺口：虽然能够记录消息、工具调用、溯源或任务依赖，却无法联合表示剩余工作、责任分配以及每个工作状态转换的证据支撑。合理的中间或最终输出可能掩盖未完成或无支撑的工作以及责任分配不当，最终影响响应质量。

Method: 提出Integrated Cooperation-Obligation REpresentation (iCORE)框架，创建统一编码X=(G,Q,Π)，包括：将可观察交互表示为合作图G，将演进工作和分配表示为义务图Q，以及审计映射Π将它们与可验证属性和证据链接。该表示使审计者能够验证两个互补属性：工作健全性（每个活跃决策相关工作断言必须通过G和Π有有限证明）和智能体分配稳定性（无可行替代智能体在评估义务上使声明贡献值改进超过ε）。建立局部到全局的健全性和分配遗憾保证。

Result: 数值结果表明，完整耦合状态能够精确重建两种执行模式下的健全性和分配缺陷；相对于被动全状态观察，iCORE-Audit在受控和真实LLM执行中分别产生11.5%和26.4%的绝对轨迹质量改进，以及15.1%和31.0%的绝对终端性能改进。建立了在给定条件下的性能界限。

Conclusion: iCORE作为工作流上的工具层，有效填补了LLM-agent系统的可审计性缺口，通过统一表示工作状态、责任分配和证据支撑，实现了对工作健全性和智能体分配稳定性的验证，显著提升了系统执行质量。

Abstract: LLM-agent systems can solve complex tasks through dynamic self-organization and emergent cooperation. Auditing this process is essential because plausible intermediate or final outputs can conceal incomplete or unsupported work and poorly allocated responsibility, ultimately compromising response quality. While existing approaches may record messages, tool calls, provenance, or task dependencies, an auditability gap exists as they do not jointly represent what work remains, who is responsible for it, and what evidence justifies each work-state transition. We address this auditability gap by proposing \emph{Integrated Cooperation-Obligation REpresentation} (iCORE). It creates a unified encoding $X=(G,Q,Π)$ integrating observable interactions as a cooperation graph $G$, evolving work and assignments as an obligation graph $Q$, and the audit map $Π$ linking them with verifiable properties and evidence. This iCORE representation enables the auditor to certify two complementary properties: {Work soundness}, where every active decision-relevant work assertion must have a finite justification through $G$ and $Π$; and {Agent-assignment stability}, which requires that no feasible alternative agent improve the declared contribution value for an evaluated obligation by more than $ε$. We establish local-to-global soundness and assignment-regret guarantees and a performance bound under stated conditions. iCORE is an instrumentation layer over workflows. Numerical results show that the full coupled state exactly reconstructs soundness and assignment defects in two execution modes and that, relative to passive full-state observation, iCORE-Audit yields absolute trajectory-quality improvements of $11.5\%$ and $26.4\%$ in controlled and real-LLM execution, respectively, with corresponding absolute terminal-performance improvements of $15.1\%$ and $31.0\%$.

</details>


### [256] [Stop Shipping AI Agents on Faith: Capability Is Not Production Readiness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27677)
*Fouad Bousetouane*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了ProofAgent Index（PAI），这是一个用于AI代理的生产就绪性治理指数，通过评估、上下文、合规和治理四个维度来量化代理是否准备好在生产约束下运行，而非仅仅依赖能力测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI代理正进入生产工作流程，但发布决策仍依赖于能力指标、演示或行为测试，这些都无法证明代理是否准备好在生产约束下运行。能力不等于生产就绪性，需要一种系统化的治理就绪性评估方法。

Method: 引入ProofAgent Index（PAI），结合四个部署证据维度：评估（测量观察到的行为）、上下文（测量影响行为的运行环境）、合规（测量与适用规则和控制的一致性）和治理（测量组织是否能在运行期间授权、监控、审计和控制代理）。在开源基础设施ProofAgent Harness中实现该指数。

Result: 在医疗和金融两个高度监管领域进行验证，表明PAI能够保留留出的就绪信号并区分高风险和低风险配置。上下文工程显著改变可靠性，能力改善行为但不决定就绪性，治理证据必须保持可见而非被平均消除。

Conclusion: PAI将代理发布从基于信任的部署决策重新定义为可审计的就绪性决策，为AI代理的生产部署提供了系统化的治理框架。

Abstract: AI agents are moving into production workflows where they retrieve information, call tools, maintain state, and act on behalf of users or organizations, but many release decisions still rely on capability signals, demos, or behavioral tests that do not show whether an agent is ready to operate under production constraints. Capability is therefore not production readiness. This paper introduces the ProofAgent Index (PAI), a governance readiness index for AI agents. PAI combines four dimensions of deployment evidence: Evaluation, Context, Compliance, and Governance. Evaluation measures observed behavior, Context measures the operating environment that shapes that behavior, Compliance measures alignment with applicable rules and controls, and Governance measures whether the organization can authorize, monitor, audit, and control the agent during operation. PAI is implemented inside ProofAgent Harness, an open source infrastructure for auditable AI agent evaluation and governance. Validation across two heavily regulated domains, healthcare and finance, shows that PAI carries held out readiness signal and separates higher risk from lower risk configurations. The results show that context engineering strongly changes reliability, capability improves behavior but does not determine readiness, and governance evidence must remain visible rather than averaged away. PAI reframes agent release from a faith based deployment decision into an auditable readiness decision.

</details>


### [257] [Scaling LLM-Driven Multi-Agent Systems: Design Principles and Architectural Scalability Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27942)
*Linus Sander,Fengjunjie Pan,Vahid Zolfaghari,Andre Schamschurko,Nenad Petrovic,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的四个可扩展设计原则，并评估了不同复杂度配置，发现只有在LLM能力超过阈值时系统扩展才能提升性能，一致性问题是主要挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于LLM的多智能体系统在理论上具有实现集体智能和解决复杂任务的潜力，但其架构设计空间尚未系统化，缺乏广泛确立的设计原则，且系统的可扩展性特征仅被部分理解。

Method: 从先前工作的结构化分析中提炼出四个设计原则（简单性、弹性反馈、带可选循环的顺序工作流、基于摘要的通信），将这些原则操作化为参考架构（拓扑形式化为约束有向工作流图），并在标准化终端系统工程任务基准上使用两种不同能力的LLM评估四种递增复杂度的配置。

Result: 扩展能带来可测量的准确率提升，成本呈近似线性增长，但仅在底层LLM超过最低能力阈值时有效；性能在中等复杂度达到峰值后因超时和评估限制而下降；一致性问题是所有扩展层级的核心挑战。

Conclusion: 为实践者提供了具体的设计指导，并强调一致性和评估标准化是未来研究的关键目标。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems have the potential to enable collective intelligence and scale toward solving highly complex tasks through coordinated ensembles of specialized agents. However, despite their theoretical potential, the architectural design space remains largely non-systematized and lacks broadly established design principles. Furthermore, the scalability characteristics of such systems are only partially understood so far. This paper makes two contributions. We first distill four design principles for scalable MAS architectures from a structured analysis of prior work: simplicity, elastic feedback, sequential workflows with optional loops, and summary-based communication. We operationalize these principles in a reference architecture whose topology is formalized as a constrained directed workflow graph, and we evaluate four configurations of increasing complexity on a standardized benchmark of terminal-based system engineering tasks using two LLMs of differing capability. Our findings show that scaling yields measurable accuracy improvements with approximately linear cost growth, but only when the underlying LLM exceeds a minimum capability threshold. Performance peaks at intermediate complexity, then degrades due to timeouts and evaluation limitations. In addition, persistent consistency issues emerge as a central challenge across all scaling levels. These results provide concrete design guidance for practitioners and highlight consistency and evaluation standardization as key targets for future research.

</details>


### [258] [Argonaut: Interactive Visual Exploration for Distributed Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27946)
*Srijoni Majumdar,Chuhao Qin,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了Argonaut，一个轻量级、容器化的优化仪表板，用于在去中心化环境中交互式地可视化探索多智能体离散选择优化的整个搜索过程，使优化过程从黑箱计算转变为人机交互过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化环境下的分布式离散选择优化中，随着系统规模扩展，理解其他智能体的选择、选择间的相互依赖关系以及如何共同达成全局目标变得极其复杂。现有方法主要是集中式的，仅可视化最终解决方案或基于固定数据集提供算法后端，导致解决方案的生成过程成为一个黑箱，缺乏对搜索过程的可观察性。

Method: 提出Argonaut，一个轻量级、容器化的优化仪表板。它允许用户上传数据集、构建智能体和选项、实时修改决策空间和参数，并运行多种算法后端来检查配置如何影响局部智能体决策和全局目标。系统采用Node.js界面，具有可扩展的Java和Python优化后端，将系统构建、优化和分析统一在一个交互循环中。

Result: 在真实世界的家庭用电、共享出行和传感器数据交换数据集上评估了Argonaut，规模扩展至5600个智能体，在暴力搜索下生成多达100万个解决方案。对于包含100个决策属性的200个智能体，典型运行时间保持在30秒以内。

Conclusion: Argonaut首次将系统构建、优化和分析整合在一个交互循环中，使分布式离散选择优化成为一个有人参与的交互过程，而非一次性的黑箱计算，为理解和导航去中心化优化问题提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Distributed discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings is often hard to explore and navigate: disentangling what other agents choose, how their choices are interdependent, and how they collectively reach a global objective quickly becomes intractable as the system scales. The major limitation is observability of the search process. Existing methods are largely centralized and offer limited support, visualizing only the final solution or providing algorithm backends over a fixed dataset, so how a solution is reached stays a black box. We present Argonaut, a lightweight, containerized optimization dashboard that enables interactive, visual exploration of the entire search process for multi-agent discrete-choice optimization in decentralized settings. Users upload datasets, construct agents and options, modify the decision space and its parameters on the fly, and run multiple algorithm backends to inspect how each configuration shapes local agent decisions and the resulting global objective. By uniting system construction, optimization, and analysis in one interactive loop, the first of its kind, Argonaut makes distributed discrete-choice optimization a human-in-the-loop process rather than a one-shot, black-box computation. We evaluate Argonaut on real-world household-electricity, shared-mobility, and sensor-data-exchange datasets scaling to 5600 agents and up to 1M solutions under brute force. Built on a Node.js interface with extensible Java and Python optimization backends, it maintains a typical runtime of 200 agents over 100 decision attributes in under 30 seconds.

</details>


### [259] [$Σ$-Mem: An Online Reliability Memory for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27958)
*Peilin Feng,Suorong Yang,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了Σ-Mem，一种在线可靠性记忆系统，通过记录历史能力证据和同伴关系证据来评估多智能体系统中各智能体的可信度，实现了自适应协调和优于多数投票的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体记忆系统主要保存交互内容，缺乏对智能体可信度及其适用条件的建模。在多智能体系统中，中央模型无法直接验证看似合理或相关的同伴响应，这限制了对智能体可靠性的有效评估。

Method: 引入Σ-Mem在线可靠性记忆系统，将历史能力证据和同伴关系证据维护为实对称状态，通过决策后正确性反馈进行更新。利用Weyl不等式确保事件级更新引起的谱变化有界，实现无需重训模型的稳定在线适应。提供通用读写接口，支持残差引导、响应无关同伴路由和可靠性加权投票。

Result: 在五个Qwen家族模型上的实验表明，Σ-Mem能适应反事实可靠性变化，泛化到未见过的智能体和任务领域。在完整OOD评估集上，直接记忆读取优于多数投票和最佳固定同伴。随着正确性反馈增多，性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 可靠性记忆作为LLM多智能体系统中自适应协调的可复用基础具有有效性，Σ-Mem能够渐进式累积可操作的可靠性信息并提升系统性能。

Abstract: Memory is central to long-horizon LLM agents, yet existing memory systems primarily preserve interaction content rather than modeling which agents can be trusted and under what conditions. This limitation is particularly important in multi-agent systems, where a central model may be unable to directly verify plausible or correlated peer responses. We introduce $Σ$-Mem, an online reliability memory that records historical competence evidence for individual peers and peer relationship evidence across the peer set. Both forms of evidence are maintained as real symmetric states and updated from post-decision correctness feedback. By Weyl's inequality, the spectral change caused by each event-level update is bounded, enabling stable online adaptation without retraining the underlying models. $Σ$-Mem provides a general write-and-read interface: the same memory can be used for residual steering of a central model, response-free peer routing, or reliability-weighted voting. Across five Qwen-family models, $Σ$-Mem adapts to counterfactual reliability shifts and generalizes to unseen peers and task domains. Direct memory readouts also outperform majority voting and the best fixed peer over the full OOD evaluation set. Moreover, performance improves consistently as more correctness feedback becomes available, indicating that $Σ$-Mem progressively accumulates actionable reliability information. These results establish reliability memory as a reusable foundation for adaptive coordination in LLM-based multi-agent systems.

</details>


### [260] [VISA: A Structured Description Protocol for Agent-Based Simulation Models Towards Machine Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28027)
*Zhou He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了VISA协议，通过8个互联表格结构化描述基于代理模型(ABM)，解决其难以复现的问题，实现了模型的有效性检查和LLM辅助的自动代码生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于代理模型(ABM)存在严重的可复现性问题：模型行为分散在文本叙述、平台特定代码和隐含假设中，导致不同读者从同一文档重建出不同模型，阻碍了科学研究的可重复性。

Method: 提出VISA结构化描述协议，包含八个互联表格（四个代理层面：Agent、Variable、Sensing、Internal Function；四个模型层面：Associated Data、Input/Output、Schedule、Validation）。遵循最小完备性原则，提供十九个可执行一致性规则和三个LLA可执行技能（撰写、检查、代码生成），实现完整的作者-检查-代码-复现循环。

Result: 在三个独立开发、跨平台的ABM上验证了VISA协议：成功将两个NetLogo模型直接从VISA规范复现为Python代码；完整捕获了一个工业级AnyLogic模型的八表格表示并通过全部十九条规则，同时明确标识了因专有移动库和数据缺失导致的复现障碍。

Conclusion: VISA将ABM的可复现障碍从不可见的模型层面转移到了可命名、可定位的依赖层面，使其变得可操作，显著提升了模型描述的明确性、可检查性和可复现性，为科学计算研究提供了重要的基础设施支持。

Abstract: Agent-based models (ABMs) are difficult to reproduce: their behavior is spread across prose narratives, platform-specific code, and implicit assumptions, so that two readers routinely reconstruct different models from the same documentation. We present VISA, a structured, symbol-based description protocol that specifies a model in eight interconnected tables---four at the agent level (Agent, Variable, Sensing, Internal Function) and four at the model level (Associated Data, Input/Output, Schedule, Validation)---under the principle of minimality with completeness. VISA makes a model machine-parseable and unambiguous via two artifacts: nineteen executable consistency rules that turn model validity into a checkable property, and three reusable LLM-executable skills (authoring, checking, and code generation) that operationalize the full author--check--code--reproduce loop. We validate the protocol on three external, independently authored ABMs spanning three platforms: we reproduce two cross-language (NetLogo to Python) directly from their VISA specifications, and we capture a third, an industrial AnyLogic model, in eight tables (passing all nineteen rules) while honestly demarcating where reproduction is blocked by a proprietary movement library and unavailable data---itself a transparency contribution. VISA moves the reproduction barrier from the model, where it is invisible, to a named, localized dependency, where it is actionable.

</details>


### [261] [AgentRadio: Passive Awareness for Long-Horizon Multi-Agent Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28430)
*Xinxing Ren,Qianbo Zang,Ziyan Wang,Caelum Forder,Suman Deb,Peter Carroll,Zekun Guo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出AgentRadio异步消息传递层，使多智能体在代码理解任务中实现执行中协调，任务解决率从32.3%提升至62.1%，超越更先进的单智能体模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体在理解大型代码库时存在长时序任务能力不足的问题，单智能体在SWE-Atlas QnA基准上仅解决32.3%的任务；现有 多智能体系统仅支持阶段边界间的同步通信，无法在执行过程中实时共享发现，而代码理解子任务之间存在相互依赖性，需要执行中的动态协调。

Method: 提出AgentRadio异步消息传递层，为编码智能体框架提供三个原语：线程、消息和等待提及；其中等待提及作为后台任务运行，在不中断前台工作的情况下呈现队友消息，使每个智能体保持被动感知并将新发现融入当前任务；设计五阶段协议包括分工和协商机制。

Result: 在SWE-Atlas QnA基准上，四个由AgentRadio组织的智能体解决62.1%的任务，比单智能体提升29.8个百分点，超过使用更新Opus 4.8的Claude Code（57.2%）；评分级别分析显示收益随任务难度增加而增长，验证了中期修正作为底层机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 异步消息传递机制使多智能体能够在执行过程中实时协调而非仅在阶段边界交接，显著提升了长时序代码理解任务的解决能力；收益随任务难度增加的趋势表明执行中协调是解决复杂任务的关键机制。

Abstract: Understanding large codebases is a long-horizon task for Large Language Model (LLM) agents: answering a single question can require building and running the software, tracing execution across files, and synthesizing evidence over tens of minutes. On SWE-Atlas QnA, a benchmark of long-horizon questions over production repositories, a single Claude Code agent (Opus 4.6) resolves only 32.3% of tasks. Dividing the work among agents with clean contexts mitigates this limitation. However, the subtasks of code comprehension are interdependent. One agent's findings can rewrite another's task, so agents must coordinate during execution, not only at phase boundaries. Existing multi-agent systems support such exchange only between phases, through staged handoffs or synchronized rounds. Communication and work remain mutually exclusive. A discovery made mid-execution cannot be shared until the next boundary. We present AgentRadio, an asynchronous message-passing layer that equips coding-agent harnesses with three primitives: threads, messages, and waiting for mentions. The last runs as a background task, surfacing teammates' messages without interrupting foreground work, so each agent remains passively aware of its peers and folds new findings into its ongoing task. Under a five-phase protocol of division of labor and negotiation, four agents organized by AgentRadio resolve 62.1% of tasks, 29.8 points above a single agent and above Claude Code with the newer Opus 4.8 (57.2%). Rubric-level analysis shows the gain growing with task difficulty, consistent with mid-course correction as the underlying mechanism. Our code is available at https://github.com/Coral-Protocol/AgentRadio.

</details>


### [262] [Using Theory of Mind to Arbitrate between Social and Non-social Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28601)
*Lance Ying,Ryan Truong,Joshua B. Tenenbaum,Samuel J. Gershman*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本研究提出了理性归心模型来解释人们如何在社交学习和非社交学习之间做出选择，通过实验验证了该模型能够定量捕捉人类在这两种策略间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然社交学习是一种强大的学习机制，但人类有时会选择付出时间和认知资源成本的直接经验而非社交学习。理解人们如何平衡社交和非社交学习策略是核心动机。

Method: 提出理性归心模型，通过推理其他代理的目标及其未来行动的信息性来估算社交学习的效用，并将其与非社交学习的效用进行权衡。设计了一款新颖的游戏，让玩家在观察其他代理或探索环境之间做出选择。

Result: 理性归心模型能够定量地捕捉人类在社交学习和非社交学习策略之间的权衡，实验数据支持模型预测。

Conclusion: 选择性社交学习受到服务于效用最大化的心理理论的指导，表明心智理论在社交学习决策中发挥关键作用。

Abstract: Social learning is a powerful mechanism through which agents learn about the world from others. However, humans sometimes choose direct experience over social learning, which can carry time and cognitive resource costs. How do people balance social and non-social learning? We propose a Rational Mentalizing model of the decision to engage in social learning. This model estimates the utility of social learning by reasoning about another agent's goal and the informativeness of their future actions. It then weighs the utility of social learning against the utility of non-social learning. Using a novel game where players choose between observing other agents or exploring the environment, we show that the Rational Mentalizing model can quantitatively capture human trade-offs between these strategies. These findings suggest that selective social learning is guided by 'Theory of Mind' in the service of utility maximization.

</details>
